    (() => {
      const W = 900;
      const H = 600;
      const STEP = 1 / 120;
      const MIN_COMPONENT = 60;
      const ASSIST_SPEED = 420;
      const BALL_FLOOR_PAD = 8;
      const EVENT_LIMIT = 12;
    
      const clone = (value) => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
      const clamp = (value, min, max) => Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
      const sign = (value, fallback = 1) => (value < 0 ? -1 : value > 0 ? 1 : 
fallback);
    
      let catalog = null;
      let levelMap = new Map();
      let brickRows = new Map();
      let constants = new Map();
    
      function constant(name, fallback) {
        const value = constants.get(name);
        if (value === undefined || value === null || value === '') return 
fallback;
        const n = Number(value);
        return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : value;
      }
    
      function configure(data) {
        catalog = data || {};
        levelMap = new Map((data.levels || []).map((row) => [Number(row.level), 
row]));
        brickRows = new Map();
        for (const row of data.bricks || []) {
          const list = brickRows.get(Number(row.level)) || [];
          list.push(row);
          brickRows.set(Number(row.level), list);
        }
        constants = new Map((data.constants || []).map((row) => 
[String(row.key), row.value]));
      }
    
      function getLevel(level) {
        return levelMap.get(Number(level)) || { level: Number(level), name: 
`Level ${level}`, base_speed: 300, speed_cap: 520, accent: '#ffffff' };
      }
    
      function brickLayoutForLevel(level) {
        const rows = brickRows.get(Number(level)) || [];
        const maxRow = rows.reduce((max, row) => Math.max(max, Number(row.row)),
1);
        const maxCol = rows.reduce((max, row) => Math.max(max, 
Number(row.column)), 1);
        const gapX = 6;
        const gapY = 6;
        const top = 74;
        const available = 900 - 96;
        const width = Math.floor((available - gapX * (maxCol - 1)) / maxCol);
        const height = 24;
        const totalWidth = maxCol * width + (maxCol - 1) * gapX;
        const startX = Math.floor((900 - totalWidth) / 2);
        return rows.map((row) => ({
          id: `${level}-${row.row}-${row.column}`,
          level: Number(row.level),
          row: Number(row.row),
          column: Number(row.column),
          type: row.type,
          drop: row.drop || '',
          hp: row.type === 'strong' ? 2 : row.type === 'solid' ? Infinity : 1,
          damaged: false,
          destroyed: false,
          x: startX + (Number(row.column) - 1) * (width + gapX),
          y: top + (Number(row.row) - 1) * (height + gapY),
          width,
          height,
        }));
      }
    
      function createEmptyEvents() {
        return [];
      }
    
      function pushEvent(state, message) {
        if (!message) return;
        state.events = state.events || [];
        state.events.unshift(message);
        if (state.events.length > EVENT_LIMIT) state.events.length = 
EVENT_LIMIT;
      }
    
      function createBaseState(levelNumber, selectedLevel, runId) {
        const level = getLevel(levelNumber);
        const paddleWidth = Number(constant('paddle_width', 118));
        const paddleHeight = Number(constant('paddle_height', 18));
        const ballRadius = Number(constant('ball_radius', 9));
        const baseSpeed = Number(level.base_speed || 300);
        const speedCap = Number(level.speed_cap || 520);
        const paddleX = Math.round(W / 2 - paddleWidth / 2);
        const bricks = brickLayoutForLevel(levelNumber);
        return {
          runId: runId || `run-${cryptoRandomHex(16)}`,
          revision: 0,
          mode: 'ready',
          phase: 'ready',
          level: Number(levelNumber),
          levelName: level.name,
          selectedLevel: Number(selectedLevel || levelNumber),
          baseSpeed,
          speedCap,
          score: 0,
          lives: Number(constant('initial_lives', 3)),
          combo: 1,
          nextExtraLife: Number(constant('extra_life_step', 20000)),
          activePower: null,
          paddle: {
            x: paddleX,
            y: H - 60,
            width: paddleWidth,
            height: paddleHeight,
            vx: 0,
            targetX: paddleX,
          },
          balls: [
            {
              id: `${runId || 'run'}-ball-1`,
              primary: true,
              state: 'waiting',
              x: paddleX + paddleWidth / 2,
              y: H - 68,
              vx: 0,
              vy: 0,
              radius: ballRadius,
              releaseX: 0,
              releaseY: -baseSpeed,
            },
          ],
          drops: [],
          bricks,
          stepCount: 0,
          simulationSeconds: 0,
          assistPaddle: false,
          launched: false,
          manualControl: false,
          events: createEmptyEvents(),
          terminal: null,
          pendingLaunch: false,
          pauseHint: 'ready',
        };
      }
    
      function cryptoRandomHex(bytes) {
        const buf = new Uint8Array(bytes);
        if (window.crypto && window.crypto.getRandomValues) {
          window.crypto.getRandomValues(buf);
        } else {
          for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i += 1) buf[i] = 
Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
        }
        return Array.from(buf, (value) => value.toString(16).padStart(2, 
'0')).join('');
      }
    
      function normalizeSnapshot(snapshot) {
        const state = clone(snapshot || {});
        state.events = Array.isArray(state.events) ? state.events : [];
        state.drops = Array.isArray(state.drops) ? state.drops : [];
        state.balls = Array.isArray(state.balls) ? state.balls : [];
        state.bricks = Array.isArray(state.bricks) ? state.bricks : [];
        state.paddle = state.paddle || { x: 0, y: H - 60, width: 
Number(constant('paddle_width', 118)), height: Number(constant('paddle_height', 
18)), vx: 0, targetX: 0 };
        state.activePower = state.activePower || null;
        state.combo = Number(state.combo || 1);
        state.score = Number(state.score || 0);
        state.lives = Number(state.lives || 0);
        state.level = Number(state.level || 1);
        state.levelName = state.levelName || getLevel(state.level).name;
        state.baseSpeed = Number(state.baseSpeed || 
getLevel(state.level).base_speed || 300);
        state.speedCap = Number(state.speedCap || 
getLevel(state.level).speed_cap || 520);
        state.nextExtraLife = Number(state.nextExtraLife || 
constant('extra_life_step', 20000));
        state.revision = Number(state.revision || 0);
        state.stepCount = Number(state.stepCount || 0);
        state.simulationSeconds = Number(state.simulationSeconds || 0);
        state.selectedLevel = Number(state.selectedLevel || state.level || 1);
        state.assistPaddle = Boolean(state.assistPaddle);
        state.mode = state.mode || state.phase || 'paused';
        state.phase = state.phase || state.mode;
        state.terminal = state.terminal || null;
        state.manualControl = Boolean(state.manualControl);
        state.pendingLaunch = Boolean(state.pendingLaunch);
        return state;
      }
    
      function setMode(state, mode, message) {
        state.mode = mode;
        state.phase = mode;
        state.pauseHint = mode;
        if (message) pushEvent(state, message);
      }
    
      function ensureBallStates(state) {
        if (!state.balls.length) return;
        const primary = state.balls.find((ball) => ball.primary && ball.state 
!== 'lost');
        if (primary) return;
        const fallback = state.balls.find((ball) => ball.state !== 'lost');
        if (fallback) fallback.primary = true;
      }
    
      function currentPrimaryBall(state) {
        return state.balls.find((ball) => ball.primary && ball.state !== 'lost')
|| state.balls.find((ball) => ball.state !== 'lost');
      }
    
      function removeLostBalls(state) {
        state.balls = state.balls.filter((ball) => ball.state !== 'lost');
        ensureBallStates(state);
      }
    
      function restoreSpeedToLevel(state, ball) {
        const minSpeed = Number(state.baseSpeed || 
getLevel(state.level).base_speed || 300);
        const cap = Number(state.speedCap || getLevel(state.level).speed_cap || 
520);
        const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) || minSpeed;
        const target = clamp(speed / 0.7, minSpeed, cap);
        const ratio = target / (speed || 1);
        ball.vx *= ratio;
        ball.vy *= ratio;
        clampBallSpeed(state, ball);
      }
    
      function clampBallSpeed(state, ball) {
        const cap = Number(state.speedCap || getLevel(state.level).speed_cap || 
520);
        let speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
        if (!speed) return;
        if (speed > cap) {
          const ratio = cap / speed;
          ball.vx *= ratio;
          ball.vy *= ratio;
          speed = cap;
        }
        const minComponent = Math.min(MIN_COMPONENT, speed * 0.35);
        if (Math.abs(ball.vx) < minComponent) ball.vx = sign(ball.vx || 
ball.releaseX || 1) * minComponent;
        if (Math.abs(ball.vy) < minComponent) ball.vy = sign(ball.vy || -1, -1) 
* minComponent;
        const adjusted = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
        if (adjusted > cap) {
          const ratio = cap / adjusted;
          ball.vx *= ratio;
          ball.vy *= ratio;
        }
      }
    
      function clearEffect(state, reason) {
        if (!state.activePower) return;
        if (state.activePower.type === 'wide') {
          state.paddle.width = Number(constant('paddle_width', 118));
          state.paddle.x = clamp(state.paddle.x, 0, W - state.paddle.width);
        }
        if (state.activePower.type === 'slow') {
          for (const ball of state.balls) {
            if (ball.state === 'moving') restoreSpeedToLevel(state, ball);
          }
        }
        if (state.activePower.type === 'multiball') {
          const primary = currentPrimaryBall(state);
          state.balls = primary ? state.balls.filter((ball) => ball === primary 
|| ball.state === 'waiting' || ball.state === 'held') : state.balls.slice(0, 1);
          ensureBallStates(state);
        }
        if (state.activePower.type === 'sticky') {
          for (const ball of state.balls) {
            if (ball.state === 'held') {
              releaseHeldBall(state, ball, reason || 'Sticky expired');
            }
          }
        }
        state.activePower = null;
      }
    
      function applyPower(state, type, source) {
        if (!type) return;
        if (state.activePower && state.activePower.type === type) {
          state.activePower.remaining = 
Number(constant('powerup_duration_seconds', 20));
          pushEvent(state, `${type} refreshed`);
          return;
        }
        if (state.activePower) clearEffect(state, `Replaced by ${type}`);
        state.activePower = { type, remaining: 
Number(constant('powerup_duration_seconds', 20)) };
        if (type === 'wide') {
          state.paddle.width = Math.round(Number(constant('paddle_width', 118)) 
* 1.5);
          state.paddle.x = clamp(state.paddle.x - Math.round((state.paddle.width
- Number(constant('paddle_width', 118))) / 2), 0, W - state.paddle.width);
        }
        if (type === 'slow') {
          for (const ball of state.balls) {
            if (ball.state === 'moving') {
              ball.vx *= 0.7;
              ball.vy *= 0.7;
              clampBallSpeed(state, ball);
            }
          }
        }
        if (type === 'multiball') {
          const primary = currentPrimaryBall(state);
          if (primary && !state.balls.some((ball) => !ball.primary && ball.state
!== 'lost')) {
            const extra = clone(primary);
            extra.id = `${primary.id}-extra-${cryptoRandomHex(4)}`;
            extra.primary = false;
            extra.x += 12;
            extra.vx = -primary.vx || 120;
            extra.vy = primary.vy;
            clampBallSpeed(state, extra);
            state.balls.push(extra);
          }
        }
        if (type === 'sticky') {
          for (const ball of state.balls) {
            if (ball.state === 'held') {
              ball.releaseAfterHold = true;
            }
          }
        }
        pushEvent(state, `${source || type} collected`);
      }
    
      function applyScore(state, basePoints) {
        const gained = basePoints * state.combo;
        state.score += gained;
        pushEvent(state, `+${gained} at x${state.combo}`);
        state.combo = Math.min(5, state.combo + 1);
        while (state.score >= state.nextExtraLife) {
          state.lives += 1;
          state.nextExtraLife += Number(constant('extra_life_step', 20000));
          pushEvent(state, 'Extra life earned');
        }
      }
    
      function destroyBrick(state, brick, ball) {
        if (brick.destroyed || brick.type === 'solid') return;
        if (brick.type === 'normal') {
          applyScore(state, 100);
          brick.destroyed = true;
          brick.hp = 0;
        } else if (brick.type === 'strong') {
          if (brick.hp > 1) {
            applyScore(state, 75);
            brick.hp = 1;
            brick.damaged = true;
          } else {
            applyScore(state, 250);
            brick.destroyed = true;
            brick.hp = 0;
          }
        }
        if (brick.destroyed && brick.drop) {
          state.drops.push({ id: `${brick.id}-drop-${cryptoRandomHex(4)}`, type:
brick.drop, x: brick.x + brick.width / 2 - 10, y: brick.y + brick.height / 2, 
vy: 120 });
        }
        pushEvent(state, `${brick.type} brick hit`);
        reflectBallFromBrick(ball, brick);
        if (brick.destroyed && !state.bricks.some((candidate) => candidate.type 
!== 'solid' && !candidate.destroyed)) {
          completeLevel(state);
        }
      }
    
      function reflectBallFromBrick(ball, brick) {
        const cx = ball.x;
        const cy = ball.y;
        const rx = brick.x;
        const ry = brick.y;
        const rw = brick.width;
        const rh = brick.height;
        const nearestX = clamp(cx, rx, rx + rw);
        const nearestY = clamp(cy, ry, ry + rh);
        let nx = cx - nearestX;
        let ny = cy - nearestY;
        if (nx === 0 && ny === 0) {
          const left = Math.abs(cx - rx);
          const right = Math.abs(cx - (rx + rw));
          const top = Math.abs(cy - ry);
          const bottom = Math.abs(cy - (ry + rh));
          const side = Math.min(left, right, top, bottom);
          if (side === left) nx = -1;
          else if (side === right) nx = 1;
          else if (side === top) ny = -1;
          else ny = 1;
        }
        const length = Math.hypot(nx, ny) || 1;
        nx /= length;
        ny /= length;
        const dot = ball.vx * nx + ball.vy * ny;
        ball.vx -= 2 * dot * nx;
        ball.vy -= 2 * dot * ny;
        ball.x = nearestX + nx * (ball.radius + 0.5);
        ball.y = nearestY + ny * (ball.radius + 0.5);
        clampBallSpeed({ ...stateFor(ball), speedCap: Number(ball.speedCap || 
520) }, ball);
      }
    
      function stateFor(ball) {
        return { level: ball.level || 1, baseSpeed: ball.baseSpeed || 300, 
speedCap: ball.speedCap || 520 };
      }
    
      function reflectBallFromPaddle(state, ball) {
        const paddle = state.paddle;
        const center = paddle.x + paddle.width / 2;
        const offset = clamp((ball.x - center) / (paddle.width / 2), -1, 1);
        const baseSpeed = Number(state.baseSpeed || 
getLevel(state.level).base_speed || 300);
        const cap = Number(state.speedCap || getLevel(state.level).speed_cap || 
520);
        const speed = clamp(Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) || baseSpeed, 
baseSpeed, cap);
        let vx = offset * speed * 0.78 + paddle.vx * 0.28;
        let vy = -Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, speed * speed - vx * vx));
        if (Math.abs(vx) < MIN_COMPONENT) vx = sign(vx || offset || 1) * 
MIN_COMPONENT;
        if (Math.abs(vy) < MIN_COMPONENT) vy = -MIN_COMPONENT;
        const norm = Math.hypot(vx, vy) || speed;
        ball.vx = vx * (speed / norm);
        ball.vy = vy * (speed / norm);
        clampBallSpeed(state, ball);
        pushEvent(state, 'Paddle contact');
        state.combo = 1;
      }
    
      function releaseHeldBall(state, ball, reason) {
        ball.state = 'moving';
        const baseSpeed = Number(state.baseSpeed || 
getLevel(state.level).base_speed || 300);
        const angle = ball.releaseX || 0;
        ball.vx = clamp(angle * 0.9, -baseSpeed * 0.75, baseSpeed * 0.75);
        ball.vy = -Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, baseSpeed * baseSpeed - ball.vx * 
ball.vx));
        clampBallSpeed(state, ball);
        pushEvent(state, reason || 'Sticky release');
      }
    
      function captureBallOnPaddle(state, ball) {
        ball.state = 'held';
        ball.vx = 0;
        ball.vy = 0;
        ball.y = state.paddle.y - ball.radius - 1;
        ball.x = state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2;
        ball.releaseX = 0;
        pushEvent(state, 'Sticky capture');
      }
    
      function checkPowerExpiry(state, dt) {
        if (!state.activePower) return;
        state.activePower.remaining -= dt;
        if (state.activePower.remaining > 0) return;
        if (state.activePower.type === 'sticky') {
          for (const ball of state.balls) {
            if (ball.state === 'held') releaseHeldBall(state, ball, 'Sticky 
expired');
          }
        }
        if (state.activePower.type === 'multiball') {
          const primary = currentPrimaryBall(state);
          state.balls = primary ? state.balls.filter((ball) => ball === primary 
|| ball.state === 'waiting' || ball.state === 'held') : state.balls.slice(0, 1);
          ensureBallStates(state);
          pushEvent(state, 'Multiball expired');
        }
        if (state.activePower.type === 'wide') {
          state.paddle.width = Number(constant('paddle_width', 118));
          state.paddle.x = clamp(state.paddle.x, 0, W - state.paddle.width);
          pushEvent(state, 'Wide expired');
        }
        if (state.activePower.type === 'slow') {
          for (const ball of state.balls) {
            if (ball.state === 'moving') restoreSpeedToLevel(state, ball);
          }
          pushEvent(state, 'Slow expired');
        }
        state.activePower = null;
      }
    
      function collectDrops(state, dt) {
        const paddle = state.paddle;
        const reachable = state.assistPaddle ? ASSIST_SPEED : null;
        for (const drop of state.drops) {
          drop.y += drop.vy * dt;
          if (state.assistPaddle && reachable !== null) {
            const targetX = drop.x + 10 - paddle.width / 2;
            const dx = targetX - paddle.x;
            const maxMove = ASSIST_SPEED * dt;
            paddle.vx = clamp(dx, -maxMove, maxMove) / dt;
            paddle.x += clamp(dx, -maxMove, maxMove);
          }
          const catchX = paddle.x;
          const catchY = paddle.y;
          const hit = drop.x + 20 >= catchX && drop.x <= catchX + paddle.width 
&& drop.y + 20 >= catchY && drop.y <= catchY + paddle.height;
          if (hit) {
            applyPower(state, drop.type, drop.type);
            drop.collected = true;
          }
          if (drop.y > H + 30) drop.collected = true;
        }
        state.drops = state.drops.filter((drop) => !drop.collected);
      }
    
      function updatePaddle(state, input, dt, allowMove) {
        if (!allowMove) return;
        const paddle = state.paddle;
        const prevX = paddle.x;
        const moveSpeed = ASSIST_SPEED;
        let targetX = paddle.x;
        const pointerActive = Boolean(input.pointerActive);
        const left = Boolean(input.left);
        const right = Boolean(input.right);
        const manual = pointerActive || left || right;
        state.manualControl = manual;
        if (manual) state.assistPaddle = false;
        if (pointerActive && typeof input.pointerX === 'number') {
          targetX = input.pointerX - paddle.width / 2;
          paddle.x = clamp(targetX, 0, W - paddle.width);
        } else if (left || right) {
          if (left) paddle.x -= moveSpeed * dt;
          if (right) paddle.x += moveSpeed * dt;
          paddle.x = clamp(paddle.x, 0, W - paddle.width);
        } else if (state.assistPaddle) {
          const target = chooseAssistTarget(state);
          if (target !== null) {
            const desired = target - paddle.width / 2;
            const dx = desired - paddle.x;
            const delta = clamp(dx, -moveSpeed * dt, moveSpeed * dt);
            paddle.x = clamp(paddle.x + delta, 0, W - paddle.width);
          }
        }
        paddle.vx = (paddle.x - prevX) / dt;
        paddle.targetX = paddle.x;
      }
    
      function chooseAssistTarget(state) {
        const balls = state.balls.filter((ball) => ball.state === 'moving' && 
ball.vy > 0);
        const drops = state.drops.slice();
        if (balls.length) {
          balls.sort((a, b) => Math.abs((a.y + a.vy) - state.paddle.y) - 
Math.abs((b.y + b.vy) - state.paddle.y));
          return balls[0].x;
        }
        if (drops.length) {
          drops.sort((a, b) => a.y - b.y);
          return drops[0].x + 10;
        }
        return null;
      }
    
      function resolveBallPaddle(state, ball) {
        const paddle = state.paddle;
        if (ball.vy <= 0) return false;
        const withinX = ball.x + ball.radius >= paddle.x && ball.x - ball.radius
<= paddle.x + paddle.width;
        const withinY = ball.y + ball.radius >= paddle.y && ball.y - ball.radius
<= paddle.y + paddle.height;
        if (!withinX || !withinY) return false;
        ball.y = paddle.y - ball.radius - 0.5;
        if (state.activePower && state.activePower.type === 'sticky' && 
ball.state !== 'held') {
          captureBallOnPaddle(state, ball);
          return true;
        }
        reflectBallFromPaddle(state, ball);
        return true;
      }
    
      function resolveBallWalls(state, ball) {
        if (ball.x - ball.radius <= 0) {
          ball.x = ball.radius + 0.5;
          ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx);
        }
        if (ball.x + ball.radius >= W) {
          ball.x = W - ball.radius - 0.5;
          ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx);
        }
        if (ball.y - ball.radius <= 0) {
          ball.y = ball.radius + 0.5;
          ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy);
        }
      }
    
      function hitBrick(state, ball, brick) {
        if (brick.destroyed) return false;
        const insideX = ball.x + ball.radius >= brick.x && ball.x - ball.radius 
<= brick.x + brick.width;
        const insideY = ball.y + ball.radius >= brick.y && ball.y - ball.radius 
<= brick.y + brick.height;
        if (!insideX || !insideY) return false;
        if (brick.type === 'solid') {
          pushEvent(state, 'solid deflection');
          reflectBallFromBrick(ball, brick);
          return true;
        }
        destroyBrick(state, brick, ball);
        return true;
      }
    
      function maybePromotePrimary(state) {
        const primary = state.balls.find((ball) => ball.primary && ball.state 
!== 'lost');
        if (primary) return;
        const fallback = state.balls.find((ball) => ball.state !== 'lost');
        if (fallback) fallback.primary = true;
      }
    
      function ballDied(state, ball) {
        ball.state = 'lost';
        pushEvent(state, 'Ball lost');
        removeLostBalls(state);
        maybePromotePrimary(state);
        if (state.balls.some((candidate) => candidate.state !== 'lost')) {
          return;
        }
        state.lives -= 1;
        clearEffect(state, 'Last ball lost');
        state.drops = [];
        if (state.lives > 0) {
          const paddleWidth = state.paddle.width;
          const ballRadius = Number(constant('ball_radius', 9));
          state.balls = [
            {
              id: `${state.runId}-serve-${state.lives}-${state.stepCount}`,
              primary: true,
              state: 'waiting',
              x: state.paddle.x + paddleWidth / 2,
              y: state.paddle.y - ballRadius - 1,
              vx: 0,
              vy: 0,
              radius: ballRadius,
              releaseX: 0,
              releaseY: -state.baseSpeed,
            },
          ];
          setMode(state, 'life-lost', 'Life lost');
          return;
        }
        state.balls = [];
        state.terminal = { outcome: 'game-over' };
        setMode(state, 'game-over', 'Game over');
      }
    
      function completeLevel(state) {
        if (state.mode === 'completed' || state.mode === 'level-complete') 
return;
        const bonus = 1000 * Number(state.level || 1);
        state.score += bonus;
        pushEvent(state, `Level ${state.level} complete +${bonus}`);
        state.combo = Math.min(5, state.combo + 1);
        const nextLevel = Number(state.level) + 1;
        if (nextLevel > 10) {
          state.mode = 'completed';
          state.phase = 'completed';
          state.terminal = { outcome: 'completed' };
          state.events.unshift('Run completed');
          return;
        }
        state.mode = 'level-complete';
        state.phase = 'level-complete';
        state.terminal = null;
        state.selectedLevel = nextLevel;
        state.events.unshift(`Level ${state.level} cleared`);
      }
    
      function launchWaitingBalls(state) {
        const baseSpeed = Number(state.baseSpeed || 
getLevel(state.level).base_speed || 300);
        const waiting = state.balls.filter((ball) => ball.state === 'waiting' ||
ball.state === 'held');
        if (!waiting.length) return false;
        const primary = waiting.find((ball) => ball.primary) || waiting[0];
        for (const ball of waiting) {
          const offset = ball === primary ? 0 : (ball.primary ? 0 : (ball.x - 
(state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2)) / (state.paddle.width / 2));
          const vx = clamp(offset * baseSpeed * 0.55 + state.paddle.vx * 0.2, 
-baseSpeed * 0.75, baseSpeed * 0.75);
          ball.state = 'moving';
          ball.vx = vx;
          ball.vy = -Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, baseSpeed * baseSpeed - ball.vx * 
ball.vx));
          ball.x = ball.x || state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2;
          ball.y = state.paddle.y - ball.radius - 1;
          if (ball.primary) {
            ball.releaseX = vx / baseSpeed;
          }
          clampBallSpeed(state, ball);
        }
        state.launched = true;
        setMode(state, 'playing', 'Launch');
        return true;
      }
    
      function handleLaunchKey(state) {
        if (state.mode === 'ready' || state.mode === 'life-lost') {
          return launchWaitingBalls(state);
        }
        if (state.mode === 'paused' && state.balls.some((ball) => ball.state ===
'waiting' || ball.state === 'held')) {
          return launchWaitingBalls(state);
        }
        return false;
      }
    
      function stepOne(state, input = {}, options = {}) {
        if (!state || state.mode === 'menu') return state;
        const force = Boolean(options.force);
        if (!force && state.mode !== 'playing') {
          if (state.mode === 'ready' || state.mode === 'life-lost') {
            updatePaddle(state, input, STEP, true);
            for (const ball of state.balls) {
              if (ball.state === 'waiting' || ball.state === 'held') {
                ball.x = state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2;
                ball.y = state.paddle.y - ball.radius - 1;
              }
            }
          }
          return state;
        }
        const dt = STEP;
        state.stepCount += 1;
        state.simulationSeconds += dt;
        updatePaddle(state, input, dt, true);
        checkPowerExpiry(state, dt);
        collectDrops(state, dt);
        if (input.launch) handleLaunchKey(state);
        if (state.mode !== 'playing' && !force) return state;
        for (const ball of state.balls) {
          if (ball.state === 'waiting') {
            ball.x = state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2;
            ball.y = state.paddle.y - ball.radius - 1;
            continue;
          }
          if (ball.state === 'held') {
            ball.x = state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2;
            ball.y = state.paddle.y - ball.radius - 1;
            continue;
          }
          if (ball.state !== 'moving') continue;
          const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) || state.baseSpeed;
          const subSteps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((speed * dt) / 2.5));
          const subDt = dt / subSteps;
          let collided = false;
          for (let i = 0; i < subSteps; i += 1) {
            ball.x += ball.vx * subDt;
            ball.y += ball.vy * subDt;
            resolveBallWalls(state, ball);
            if (resolveBallPaddle(state, ball)) {
              collided = true;
              break;
            }
            const bricks = state.bricks.filter((brick) => !brick.destroyed);
            for (const brick of bricks) {
              if (hitBrick(state, ball, brick)) {
                collided = true;
                break;
              }
            }
            if (collided) break;
            if (ball.y - ball.radius > H + BALL_FLOOR_PAD) {
              ballDied(state, ball);
              collided = true;
              break;
            }
          }
          clampBallSpeed(state, ball);
          if (state.mode === 'life-lost' || state.mode === 'game-over' || 
state.mode === 'completed') break;
        }
        removeLostBalls(state);
        if (state.mode === 'playing') {
          const breakable = state.bricks.some((brick) => !brick.destroyed && 
brick.type !== 'solid');
          if (!breakable) completeLevel(state);
        }
        if (state.activePower) state.activePower.remaining = Math.max(0, 
state.activePower.remaining);
        return state;
      }
    
      function advanceForDrill(state, seconds = 1, input = {}) {
        const targetSteps = Math.min(120, Math.max(1, Math.round(seconds * 
120)));
        const initialMode = state.mode;
        state.mode = 'playing';
        state.phase = 'playing';
        for (let i = 0; i < targetSteps; i += 1) {
          stepOne(state, input, { force: true });
          if (state.mode === 'life-lost' || state.mode === 'game-over' || 
state.mode === 'level-complete' || state.mode === 'completed') {
            break;
          }
        }
        if (state.mode === 'playing') {
          state.mode = 'paused';
          state.phase = 'paused';
        }
        if (initialMode === 'paused' && state.mode === 'playing') state.mode = 
'paused';
        state.manualControl = false;
        return state;
      }
    
      function createNextLevelState(state, runId) {
        const nextLevel = Number(state.level) + 1;
        const level = getLevel(nextLevel);
        const paddleWidth = Number(constant('paddle_width', 118));
        const paddleHeight = Number(constant('paddle_height', 18));
        const ballRadius = Number(constant('ball_radius', 9));
        const baseX = Math.round(W / 2 - paddleWidth / 2);
        return {
          runId: runId || state.runId,
          revision: state.revision || 0,
          mode: 'ready',
          phase: 'ready',
          level: nextLevel,
          levelName: level.name,
          selectedLevel: nextLevel,
          baseSpeed: Number(level.base_speed || state.baseSpeed),
          speedCap: Number(level.speed_cap || state.speedCap),
          score: Number(state.score),
          lives: Number(state.lives),
          combo: Number(state.combo),
          nextExtraLife: Number(state.nextExtraLife),
          activePower: null,
          paddle: {
            x: baseX,
            y: H - 60,
            width: paddleWidth,
            height: paddleHeight,
            vx: 0,
            targetX: baseX,
          },
          balls: [
            {
              id: `${runId || state.runId}-ball-next-${cryptoRandomHex(4)}`,
              primary: true,
              state: 'waiting',
              x: baseX + paddleWidth / 2,
              y: H - 68,
              vx: 0,
              vy: 0,
              radius: ballRadius,
              releaseX: 0,
              releaseY: -Number(level.base_speed || state.baseSpeed),
            },
          ],
          drops: [],
          bricks: brickLayoutForLevel(nextLevel),
          stepCount: state.stepCount || 0,
          simulationSeconds: state.simulationSeconds || 0,
          assistPaddle: Boolean(state.assistPaddle),
          events: createEmptyEvents(),
          terminal: null,
          launched: false,
          pendingLaunch: false,
        };
      }
    
      function renderSummary(state) {
        return {
          phase: state.mode,
          score: state.score,
          lives: state.lives,
          combo: state.combo,
          balls: state.balls.map((ball) => ({
            id: ball.id,
            state: ball.state,
            primary: Boolean(ball.primary),
            x: ball.x,
            y: ball.y,
            vx: ball.vx,
            vy: ball.vy,
          })),
          items: state.drops.map((drop) => ({ id: drop.id, type: drop.type, x: 
drop.x, y: drop.y })),
          bricks: state.bricks.map((brick) => ({ id: brick.id, type: brick.type,
destroyed: Boolean(brick.destroyed), damaged: Boolean(brick.damaged), hp: 
brick.hp })),
          paddle: { width: state.paddle.width },
          effect: state.activePower ? { type: state.activePower.type, remaining:
state.activePower.remaining } : null,
          steps: state.stepCount,
          timer: Math.max(0, Math.ceil(state.activePower ? 
state.activePower.remaining : 0)),
        };
      }
    
      window.BrickfallEngine = {
        configure,
        clone,
        normalizeSnapshot,
        createBaseState,
        createNextLevelState,
        launchWaitingBalls,
        handleLaunchKey,
        stepOne,
        advanceForDrill,
        applyPower,
        clearEffect,
        pushEvent,
        renderSummary,
        getLevel,
        constant,
        brickLayoutForLevel,
      };
    })();

+18|
+19|  function constant(name, fallback) {
+20|    const value = constants.get(name);
+21|    if (value === undefined || value === null || value === '') return 
fallback;
+22|    const n = Number(value);
+23|    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : value;
+24|  }
+25|
+26|  function configure(data) {
+27|    catalog = data || {};
+28|    levelMap = new Map((data.levels || []).map((row) => [Number(row.level), 
row]));
+29|    brickRows = new Map();
+30|    for (const row of data.bricks || []) {
+31|      const list = brickRows.get(Number(row.level)) || [];
+32|      list.push(row);
+33|      brickRows.set(Number(row.level), list);
+34|    }
+35|    constants = new Map((data.constants || []).map((row) => 
[String(row.key), row.value]));
+36|  }
+37|
+38|  function getLevel(level) {
+39|    return levelMap.get(Number(level)) || { level: Number(level), name: 
`Level ${level}`, base_speed: 300, speed_cap: 520, accent: '#ffffff' };
+40|  }
+41|
+42|  function brickLayoutForLevel(level) {
+43|    const rows = brickRows.get(Number(level)) || [];
+44|    const maxRow = rows.reduce((max, row) => Math.max(max, Number(row.row)),
1);
+45|    const maxCol = rows.reduce((max, row) => Math.max(max, 
Number(row.column)), 1);
+46|    const gapX = 6;
+47|    const gapY = 6;
+48|    const top = 74;
+49|    const available = 900 - 96;
+50|    const width = Math.floor((available - gapX * (maxCol - 1)) / maxCol);
+51|    const height = 24;
+52|    const totalWidth = maxCol * width + (maxCol - 1) * gapX;
+53|    const startX = Math.floor((900 - totalWidth) / 2);
+54|    return rows.map((row) => ({
+55|      id: `${level}-${row.row}-${row.column}`,
+56|      level: Number(row.level),
+57|      row: Number(row.row),
+58|      column: Number(row.column),
+59|      type: row.type,
+60|      drop: row.drop || '',
+61|      hp: row.type === 'strong' ? 2 : row.type === 'solid' ? Infinity : 1,
+62|      damaged: false,
+63|      destroyed: false,
+64|      x: startX + (Number(row.column) - 1) * (width + gapX),
+65|      y: top + (Number(row.row) - 1) * (height + gapY),
+66|      width,
+67|      height,
+68|    }));
+69|  }
+70|
+71|  function createEmptyEvents() {
+72|    return [];
+73|  }
+74|
+75|  function pushEvent(state, message) {
+76|    if (!message) return;
+77|    state.events = state.events || [];
+78|    state.events.unshift(message);
+79|    if (state.events.length > EVENT_LIMIT) state.events.length = 
EVENT_LIMIT;
+80|  }
+81|
+82|  function createBaseState(levelNumber, selectedLevel, runId) {
+83|    const level = getLevel(levelNumber);
+84|    const paddleWidth = Number(constant('paddle_width', 118));
+85|    const paddleHeight = Number(constant('paddle_height', 18));
+86|    const ballRadius = Number(constant('ball_radius', 9));
+87|    const baseSpeed = Number(level.base_speed || 300);
+88|    const speedCap = Number(level.speed_cap || 520);
+89|    const paddleX = Math.round(W / 2 - paddleWidth / 2);
+90|    const bricks = brickLayoutForLevel(levelNumber);
+91|    return {
+92|      runId: runId || `run-${cryptoRandomHex(16)}`,
+93|      revision: 0,
+94|      mode: 'ready',
+95|      phase: 'ready',
+96|      level: Number(levelNumber),
+97|      levelName: level.name,
+98|      selectedLevel: Number(selectedLevel || levelNumber),
+99|      baseSpeed,
+100|      speedCap,
+101|      score: 0,
+102|      lives: Number(constant('initial_lives', 3)),
+103|      combo: 1,
+104|      nextExtraLife: Number(constant('extra_life_step', 20000)),
+105|      activePower: null,
+106|      paddle: {
+107|        x: paddleX,
+108|        y: H - 60,
+109|        width: paddleWidth,
+110|        height: paddleHeight,
+111|        vx: 0,
+112|        targetX: paddleX,
+113|      },
+114|      balls: [
+115|        {
+116|          id: `${runId || 'run'}-ball-1`,
+117|          primary: true,
+118|          state: 'waiting',
+119|          x: paddleX + paddleWidth / 2,
+120|          y: H - 68,
+121|          vx: 0,
+122|          vy: 0,
+123|          radius: ballRadius,
+124|          releaseX: 0,
+125|          releaseY: -baseSpeed,
+126|        },
+127|      ],
+128|      drops: [],
+129|      bricks,
+130|      stepCount: 0,
+131|      simulationSeconds: 0,
+132|      assistPaddle: false,
+133|      launched: false,
+134|      manualControl: false,
+135|      events: createEmptyEvents(),
+136|      terminal: null,
+137|      pendingLaunch: false,
+138|      pauseHint: 'ready',
+139|    };
+140|  }
+141|
+142|  function cryptoRandomHex(bytes) {
+143|    const buf = new Uint8Array(bytes);
+144|    if (window.crypto && window.crypto.getRandomValues) {
+145|      window.crypto.getRandomValues(buf);
+146|    } else {
+147|      for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i += 1) buf[i] = 
Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
+148|    }
+149|    return Array.from(buf, (value) => value.toString(16).padStart(2, 
'0')).join('');
+150|  }
+151|
+152|  function normalizeSnapshot(snapshot) {
+153|    const state = clone(snapshot || {});
+154|    state.events = Array.isArray(state.events) ? state.events : [];
+155|    state.drops = Array.isArray(state.drops) ? state.drops : [];
+156|    state.balls = Array.isArray(state.balls) ? state.balls : [];
+157|    state.bricks = Array.isArray(state.bricks) ? state.bricks : [];
+158|    state.paddle = state.paddle || { x: 0, y: H - 60, width: 
Number(constant('paddle_width', 118)), height: Number(constant('paddle_height', 
18)), vx: 0, targetX: 0 };
+159|    state.activePower = state.activePower || null;
+160|    state.combo = Number(state.combo || 1);
+161|    state.score = Number(state.score || 0);
+162|    state.lives = Number(state.lives || 0);
+163|    state.level = Number(state.level || 1);
+164|    state.levelName = state.levelName || getLevel(state.level).name;
+165|    state.baseSpeed = Number(state.baseSpeed || 
getLevel(state.level).base_speed || 300);
+166|    state.speedCap = Number(state.speedCap || 
getLevel(state.level).speed_cap || 520);
+167|    state.nextExtraLife = Number(state.nextExtraLife || 
constant('extra_life_step', 20000));
+168|    state.revision = Number(state.revision || 0);
+169|    state.stepCount = Number(state.stepCount || 0);
+170|    state.simulationSeconds = Number(state.simulationSeconds || 0);
+171|    state.selectedLevel = Number(state.selectedLevel || state.level || 1);
+172|    state.assistPaddle = Boolean(state.assistPaddle);
+173|    state.mode = state.mode || state.phase || 'paused';
+174|    state.phase = state.phase || state.mode;
+175|    state.terminal = state.terminal || null;
+176|    state.manualControl = Boolean(state.manualControl);
+177|    state.pendingLaunch = Boolean(state.pendingLaunch);
+178|    return state;
+179|  }
+180|
+181|  function setMode(state, mode, message) {
+182|    state.mode = mode;
+183|    state.phase = mode;
+184|    state.pauseHint = mode;
+185|    if (message) pushEvent(state, message);
+186|  }
+187|
+188|  function ensureBallStates(state) {
+189|    if (!state.balls.length) return;
+190|    const primary = state.balls.find((ball) => ball.primary && ball.state 
!== 'lost');
+191|    if (primary) return;
+192|    const fallback = state.balls.find((ball) => ball.state !== 'lost');
+193|    if (fallback) fallback.primary = true;
+194|  }
+195|
+196|  function currentPrimaryBall(state) {
+197|    return state.balls.find((ball) => ball.primary && ball.state !== 
'lost') || state.balls.find((ball) => ball.state !== 'lost');
+198|  }
+199|
+200|  function removeLostBalls(state) {
+201|    state.balls = state.balls.filter((ball) => ball.state !== 'lost');
+202|    ensureBallStates(state);
+203|  }
+204|
+205|  function restoreSpeedToLevel(state, ball) {
+206|    const minSpeed = Number(state.baseSpeed || 
getLevel(state.level).base_speed || 300);
+207|    const cap = Number(state.speedCap || getLevel(state.level).speed_cap ||
520);
+208|    const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) || minSpeed;
+209|    const target = clamp(speed / 0.7, minSpeed, cap);
+210|    const ratio = target / (speed || 1);
+211|    ball.vx *= ratio;
+212|    ball.vy *= ratio;
+213|    clampBallSpeed(state, ball);
+214|  }
+215|
+216|  function clampBallSpeed(state, ball) {
+217|    const cap = Number(state.speedCap || getLevel(state.level).speed_cap ||
520);
+218|    let speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
+219|    if (!speed) return;
+220|    if (speed > cap) {
+221|      const ratio = cap / speed;
+222|      ball.vx *= ratio;
+223|      ball.vy *= ratio;
+224|      speed = cap;
+225|    }
+226|    const minComponent = Math.min(MIN_COMPONENT, speed * 0.35);
+227|    if (Math.abs(ball.vx) < minComponent) ball.vx = sign(ball.vx || 
ball.releaseX || 1) * minComponent;
+228|    if (Math.abs(ball.vy) < minComponent) ball.vy = sign(ball.vy || -1, -1)
* minComponent;
+229|    const adjusted = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy);
+230|    if (adjusted > cap) {
+231|      const ratio = cap / adjusted;
+232|      ball.vx *= ratio;
+233|      ball.vy *= ratio;
+234|    }
+235|  }
+236|
+237|  function clearEffect(state, reason) {
+238|    if (!state.activePower) return;
+239|    if (state.activePower.type === 'wide') {
+240|      state.paddle.width = Number(constant('paddle_width', 118));
+241|      state.paddle.x = clamp(state.paddle.x, 0, W - state.paddle.width);
+242|    }
+243|    if (state.activePower.type === 'slow') {
+244|      for (const ball of state.balls) {
+245|        if (ball.state === 'moving') restoreSpeedToLevel(state, ball);
+246|      }
+247|    }
+248|    if (state.activePower.type === 'multiball') {
+249|      const primary = currentPrimaryBall(state);
+250|      state.balls = primary ? state.balls.filter((ball) => ball === primary
|| ball.state === 'waiting' || ball.state === 'held') : state.balls.slice(0, 1);
+251|      ensureBallStates(state);
+252|    }
+253|    if (state.activePower.type === 'sticky') {
+254|      for (const ball of state.balls) {
+255|        if (ball.state === 'held') {
+256|          releaseHeldBall(state, ball, reason || 'Sticky expired');
+257|        }
+258|      }
+259|    }
+260|    state.activePower = null;
+261|  }
+262|
+263|  function applyPower(state, type, source) {
+264|    if (!type) return;
+265|    if (state.activePower && state.activePower.type === type) {
+266|      state.activePower.remaining = 
Number(constant('powerup_duration_seconds', 20));
+267|      pushEvent(state, `${type} refreshed`);
+268|      return;
+269|    }
+270|    if (state.activePower) clearEffect(state, `Replaced by ${type}`);
+271|    state.activePower = { type, remaining: 
Number(constant('powerup_duration_seconds', 20)) };
+272|    if (type === 'wide') {
+273|      state.paddle.width = Math.round(Number(constant('paddle_width', 118))
* 1.5);
+274|      state.paddle.x = clamp(state.paddle.x - 
Math.round((state.paddle.width - Number(constant('paddle_width', 118))) / 2), 0,
W - state.paddle.width);
+275|    }
+276|    if (type === 'slow') {
+277|      for (const ball of state.balls) {
+278|        if (ball.state === 'moving') {
+279|          ball.vx *= 0.7;
+280|          ball.vy *= 0.7;
+281|          clampBallSpeed(state, ball);
+282|        }
+283|      }
+284|    }
+285|    if (type === 'multiball') {
+286|      const primary = currentPrimaryBall(state);
+287|      if (primary && !state.balls.some((ball) => !ball.primary && 
ball.state !== 'lost')) {
+288|        const extra = clone(primary);
+289|        extra.id = `${primary.id}-extra-${cryptoRandomHex(4)}`;
+290|        extra.primary = false;
+291|        extra.x += 12;
+292|        extra.vx = -primary.vx || 120;
+293|        extra.vy = primary.vy;
+294|        clampBallSpeed(state, extra);
+295|        state.balls.push(extra);
+296|      }
+297|    }
+298|    if (type === 'sticky') {
+299|      for (const ball of state.balls) {
+300|        if (ball.state === 'held') {
+301|          ball.releaseAfterHold = true;
+302|        }
+303|      }
+304|    }
+305|    pushEvent(state, `${source || type} collected`);
+306|  }
+307|
+308|  function applyScore(state, basePoints) {
+309|    const gained = basePoints * state.combo;
+310|    state.score += gained;
+311|    pushEvent(state, `+${gained} at x${state.combo}`);
+312|    state.combo = Math.min(5, state.combo + 1);
+313|    while (state.score >= state.nextExtraLife) {
+314|      state.lives += 1;
+315|      state.nextExtraLife += Number(constant('extra_life_step', 20000));
+316|      pushEvent(state, 'Extra life earned');
+317|    }
+318|  }
+319|
+320|  function destroyBrick(state, brick, ball) {
+321|    if (brick.destroyed || brick.type === 'solid') return;
+322|    if (brick.type === 'normal') {
+323|      applyScore(state, 100);
+324|      brick.destroyed = true;
+325|      brick.hp = 0;
+326|    } else if (brick.type === 'strong') {
+327|      if (brick.hp > 1) {
+328|        applyScore(state, 75);
+329|        brick.hp = 1;
+330|        brick.damaged = true;
+331|      } else {
+332|        applyScore(state, 250);
+333|        brick.destroyed = true;
+334|        brick.hp = 0;
+335|      }
+336|    }
+337|    if (brick.destroyed && brick.drop) {
+338|      state.drops.push({ id: `${brick.id}-drop-${cryptoRandomHex(4)}`, 
type: brick.drop, x: brick.x + brick.width / 2 - 10, y: brick.y + brick.height /
2, vy: 120 });
+339|    }
+340|    pushEvent(state, `${brick.type} brick hit`);
+341|    reflectBallFromBrick(ball, brick);
+342|    if (brick.destroyed && !state.bricks.some((candidate) => candidate.type
!== 'solid' && !candidate.destroyed)) {
+343|      completeLevel(state);
+344|    }
+345|  }
+346|
+347|  function reflectBallFromBrick(ball, brick) {
+348|    const cx = ball.x;
+349|    const cy = ball.y;
+350|    const rx = brick.x;
+351|    const ry = brick.y;
+352|    const rw = brick.width;
+353|    const rh = brick.height;
+354|    const nearestX = clamp(cx, rx, rx + rw);
+355|    const nearestY = clamp(cy, ry, ry + rh);
+356|    let nx = cx - nearestX;
+357|    let ny = cy - nearestY;
+358|    if (nx === 0 && ny === 0) {
+359|      const left = Math.abs(cx - rx);
+360|      const right = Math.abs(cx - (rx + rw));
+361|      const top = Math.abs(cy - ry);
+362|      const bottom = Math.abs(cy - (ry + rh));
+363|      const side = Math.min(left, right, top, bottom);
+364|      if (side === left) nx = -1;
+365|      else if (side === right) nx = 1;
+366|      else if (side === top) ny = -1;
+367|      else ny = 1;
+368|    }
+369|    const length = Math.hypot(nx, ny) || 1;
+370|    nx /= length;
+371|    ny /= length;
+372|    const dot = ball.vx * nx + ball.vy * ny;
+373|    ball.vx -= 2 * dot * nx;
+374|    ball.vy -= 2 * dot * ny;
+375|    ball.x = nearestX + nx * (ball.radius + 0.5);
+376|    ball.y = nearestY + ny * (ball.radius + 0.5);
+377|    clampBallSpeed({ ...stateFor(ball), speedCap: Number(ball.speedCap || 
520) }, ball);
+378|  }
+379|
+380|  function stateFor(ball) {
+381|    return { level: ball.level || 1, baseSpeed: ball.baseSpeed || 300, 
speedCap: ball.speedCap || 520 };
+382|  }
+383|
+384|  function reflectBallFromPaddle(state, ball) {
+385|    const paddle = state.paddle;
+386|    const center = paddle.x + paddle.width / 2;
+387|    const offset = clamp((ball.x - center) / (paddle.width / 2), -1, 1);
+388|    const baseSpeed = Number(state.baseSpeed || 
getLevel(state.level).base_speed || 300);
+389|    const cap = Number(state.speedCap || getLevel(state.level).speed_cap ||
520);
+390|    const speed = clamp(Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) || baseSpeed, 
baseSpeed, cap);
+391|    let vx = offset * speed * 0.78 + paddle.vx * 0.28;
+392|    let vy = -Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, speed * speed - vx * vx));
+393|    if (Math.abs(vx) < MIN_COMPONENT) vx = sign(vx || offset || 1) * 
MIN_COMPONENT;
+394|    if (Math.abs(vy) < MIN_COMPONENT) vy = -MIN_COMPONENT;
+395|    const norm = Math.hypot(vx, vy) || speed;
+396|    ball.vx = vx * (speed / norm);
+397|    ball.vy = vy * (speed / norm);
+398|    clampBallSpeed(state, ball);
+399|    pushEvent(state, 'Paddle contact');
+400|    state.combo = 1;
+401|  }
+402|
+403|  function releaseHeldBall(state, ball, reason) {
+404|    ball.state = 'moving';
+405|    const baseSpeed = Number(state.baseSpeed || 
getLevel(state.level).base_speed || 300);
+406|    const angle = ball.releaseX || 0;
+407|    ball.vx = clamp(angle * 0.9, -baseSpeed * 0.75, baseSpeed * 0.75);
+408|    ball.vy = -Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, baseSpeed * baseSpeed - ball.vx * 
ball.vx));
+409|    clampBallSpeed(state, ball);
+410|    pushEvent(state, reason || 'Sticky release');
+411|  }
+412|
+413|  function captureBallOnPaddle(state, ball) {
+414|    ball.state = 'held';
+415|    ball.vx = 0;
+416|    ball.vy = 0;
+417|    ball.y = state.paddle.y - ball.radius - 1;
+418|    ball.x = state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2;
+419|    ball.releaseX = 0;
+420|    pushEvent(state, 'Sticky capture');
+421|  }
+422|
+423|  function checkPowerExpiry(state, dt) {
+424|    if (!state.activePower) return;
+425|    state.activePower.remaining -= dt;
+426|    if (state.activePower.remaining > 0) return;
+427|    if (state.activePower.type === 'sticky') {
+428|      for (const ball of state.balls) {
+429|        if (ball.state === 'held') releaseHeldBall(state, ball, 'Sticky 
expired');
+430|      }
+431|    }
+432|    if (state.activePower.type === 'multiball') {
+433|      const primary = currentPrimaryBall(state);
+434|      state.balls = primary ? state.balls.filter((ball) => ball === primary
|| ball.state === 'waiting' || ball.state === 'held') : state.balls.slice(0, 1);
+435|      ensureBallStates(state);
+436|      pushEvent(state, 'Multiball expired');
+437|    }
+438|    if (state.activePower.type === 'wide') {
+439|      state.paddle.width = Number(constant('paddle_width', 118));
+440|      state.paddle.x = clamp(state.paddle.x, 0, W - state.paddle.width);
+441|      pushEvent(state, 'Wide expired');
+442|    }
+443|    if (state.activePower.type === 'slow') {
+444|      for (const ball of state.balls) {
+445|        if (ball.state === 'moving') restoreSpeedToLevel(state, ball);
+446|      }
+447|      pushEvent(state, 'Slow expired');
+448|    }
+449|    state.activePower = null;
+450|  }
+451|
+452|  function collectDrops(state, dt) {
+453|    const paddle = state.paddle;
+454|    const reachable = state.assistPaddle ? ASSIST_SPEED : null;
+455|    for (const drop of state.drops) {
+456|      drop.y += drop.vy * dt;
+457|      if (state.assistPaddle && reachable !== null) {
+458|        const targetX = drop.x + 10 - paddle.width / 2;
+459|        const dx = targetX - paddle.x;
+460|        const maxMove = ASSIST_SPEED * dt;
+461|        paddle.vx = clamp(dx, -maxMove, maxMove) / dt;
+462|        paddle.x += clamp(dx, -maxMove, maxMove);
+463|      }
+464|      const catchX = paddle.x;
+465|      const catchY = paddle.y;
+466|      const hit = drop.x + 20 >= catchX && drop.x <= catchX + paddle.width 
&& drop.y + 20 >= catchY && drop.y <= catchY + paddle.height;
+467|      if (hit) {
+468|        applyPower(state, drop.type, drop.type);
+469|        drop.collected = true;
+470|      }
+471|      if (drop.y > H + 30) drop.collected = true;
+472|    }
+473|    state.drops = state.drops.filter((drop) => !drop.collected);
+474|  }
+475|
+476|  function updatePaddle(state, input, dt, allowMove) {
+477|    if (!allowMove) return;
+478|    const paddle = state.paddle;
+479|    const prevX = paddle.x;
+480|    const moveSpeed = ASSIST_SPEED;
+481|    let targetX = paddle.x;
+482|    const pointerActive = Boolean(input.pointerActive);
+483|    const left = Boolean(input.left);
+484|    const right = Boolean(input.right);
+485|    const manual = pointerActive || left || right;
+486|    state.manualControl = manual;
+487|    if (manual) state.assistPaddle = false;
+488|    if (pointerActive && typeof input.pointerX === 'number') {
+489|      targetX = input.pointerX - paddle.width / 2;
+490|      paddle.x = clamp(targetX, 0, W - paddle.width);
+491|    } else if (left || right) {
+492|      if (left) paddle.x -= moveSpeed * dt;
+493|      if (right) paddle.x += moveSpeed * dt;
+494|      paddle.x = clamp(paddle.x, 0, W - paddle.width);
+495|    } else if (state.assistPaddle) {
+496|      const target = chooseAssistTarget(state);
+497|      if (target !== null) {
+498|        const desired = target - paddle.width / 2;
+499|        const dx = desired - paddle.x;
+500|        const delta = clamp(dx, -moveSpeed * dt, moveSpeed * dt);
+501|        paddle.x = clamp(paddle.x + delta, 0, W - paddle.width);
+502|      }
+503|    }
+504|    paddle.vx = (paddle.x - prevX) / dt;
+505|    paddle.targetX = paddle.x;
+506|  }
+507|
+508|  function chooseAssistTarget(state) {
+509|    const balls = state.balls.filter((ball) => ball.state === 'moving' && 
ball.vy > 0);
+510|    const drops = state.drops.slice();
+511|    if (balls.length) {
+512|      balls.sort((a, b) => Math.abs((a.y + a.vy) - state.paddle.y) - 
Math.abs((b.y + b.vy) - state.paddle.y));
+513|      return balls[0].x;
+514|    }
+515|    if (drops.length) {
+516|      drops.sort((a, b) => a.y - b.y);
+517|      return drops[0].x + 10;
+518|    }
+519|    return null;
+520|  }
+521|
+522|  function resolveBallPaddle(state, ball) {
+523|    const paddle = state.paddle;
+524|    if (ball.vy <= 0) return false;
+525|    const withinX = ball.x + ball.radius >= paddle.x && ball.x - 
ball.radius <= paddle.x + paddle.width;
+526|    const withinY = ball.y + ball.radius >= paddle.y && ball.y - 
ball.radius <= paddle.y + paddle.height;
+527|    if (!withinX || !withinY) return false;
+528|    ball.y = paddle.y - ball.radius - 0.5;
+529|    if (state.activePower && state.activePower.type === 'sticky' && 
ball.state !== 'held') {
+530|      captureBallOnPaddle(state, ball);
+531|      return true;
+532|    }
+533|    reflectBallFromPaddle(state, ball);
+534|    return true;
+535|  }
+536|
+537|  function resolveBallWalls(state, ball) {
+538|    if (ball.x - ball.radius <= 0) {
+539|      ball.x = ball.radius + 0.5;
+540|      ball.vx = Math.abs(ball.vx);
+541|    }
+542|    if (ball.x + ball.radius >= W) {
+543|      ball.x = W - ball.radius - 0.5;
+544|      ball.vx = -Math.abs(ball.vx);
+545|    }
+546|    if (ball.y - ball.radius <= 0) {
+547|      ball.y = ball.radius + 0.5;
+548|      ball.vy = Math.abs(ball.vy);
+549|    }
+550|  }
+551|
+552|  function hitBrick(state, ball, brick) {
+553|    if (brick.destroyed) return false;
+554|    const insideX = ball.x + ball.radius >= brick.x && ball.x - ball.radius
<= brick.x + brick.width;
+555|    const insideY = ball.y + ball.radius >= brick.y && ball.y - ball.radius
<= brick.y + brick.height;
+556|    if (!insideX || !insideY) return false;
+557|    if (brick.type === 'solid') {
+558|      pushEvent(state, 'solid deflection');
+559|      reflectBallFromBrick(ball, brick);
+560|      return true;
+561|    }
+562|    destroyBrick(state, brick, ball);
+563|    return true;
+564|  }
+565|
+566|  function maybePromotePrimary(state) {
+567|    const primary = state.balls.find((ball) => ball.primary && ball.state 
!== 'lost');
+568|    if (primary) return;
+569|    const fallback = state.balls.find((ball) => ball.state !== 'lost');
+570|    if (fallback) fallback.primary = true;
+571|  }
+572|
+573|  function ballDied(state, ball) {
+574|    ball.state = 'lost';
+575|    pushEvent(state, 'Ball lost');
+576|    removeLostBalls(state);
+577|    maybePromotePrimary(state);
+578|    if (state.balls.some((candidate) => candidate.state !== 'lost')) {
+579|      return;
+580|    }
+581|    state.lives -= 1;
+582|    clearEffect(state, 'Last ball lost');
+583|    state.drops = [];
+584|    if (state.lives > 0) {
+585|      const paddleWidth = state.paddle.width;
+586|      const ballRadius = Number(constant('ball_radius', 9));
+587|      state.balls = [
+588|        {
+589|          id: `${state.runId}-serve-${state.lives}-${state.stepCount}`,
+590|          primary: true,
+591|          state: 'waiting',
+592|          x: state.paddle.x + paddleWidth / 2,
+593|          y: state.paddle.y - ballRadius - 1,
+594|          vx: 0,
+595|          vy: 0,
+596|          radius: ballRadius,
+597|          releaseX: 0,
+598|          releaseY: -state.baseSpeed,
+599|        },
+600|      ];
+601|      setMode(state, 'life-lost', 'Life lost');
+602|      return;
+603|    }
+604|    state.balls = [];
+605|    state.terminal = { outcome: 'game-over' };
+606|    setMode(state, 'game-over', 'Game over');
+607|  }
+608|
+609|  function completeLevel(state) {
+610|    if (state.mode === 'completed' || state.mode === 'level-complete') 
return;
+611|    const bonus = 1000 * Number(state.level || 1);
+612|    state.score += bonus;
+613|    pushEvent(state, `Level ${state.level} complete +${bonus}`);
+614|    state.combo = Math.min(5, state.combo + 1);
+615|    const nextLevel = Number(state.level) + 1;
+616|    if (nextLevel > 10) {
+617|      state.mode = 'completed';
+618|      state.phase = 'completed';
+619|      state.terminal = { outcome: 'completed' };
+620|      state.events.unshift('Run completed');
+621|      return;
+622|    }
+623|    state.mode = 'level-complete';
+624|    state.phase = 'level-complete';
+625|    state.terminal = null;
+626|    state.selectedLevel = nextLevel;
+627|    state.events.unshift(`Level ${state.level} cleared`);
+628|  }
+629|
+630|  function launchWaitingBalls(state) {
+631|    const baseSpeed = Number(state.baseSpeed || 
getLevel(state.level).base_speed || 300);
+632|    const waiting = state.balls.filter((ball) => ball.state === 'waiting' 
|| ball.state === 'held');
+633|    if (!waiting.length) return false;
+634|    const primary = waiting.find((ball) => ball.primary) || waiting[0];
+635|    for (const ball of waiting) {
+636|      const offset = ball === primary ? 0 : (ball.primary ? 0 : (ball.x - 
(state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2)) / (state.paddle.width / 2));
+637|      const vx = clamp(offset * baseSpeed * 0.55 + state.paddle.vx * 0.2, 
-baseSpeed * 0.75, baseSpeed * 0.75);
+638|      ball.state = 'moving';
+639|      ball.vx = vx;
+640|      ball.vy = -Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, baseSpeed * baseSpeed - ball.vx * 
ball.vx));
+641|      ball.x = ball.x || state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2;
+642|      ball.y = state.paddle.y - ball.radius - 1;
+643|      if (ball.primary) {
+644|        ball.releaseX = vx / baseSpeed;
+645|      }
+646|      clampBallSpeed(state, ball);
+647|    }
+648|    state.launched = true;
+649|    setMode(state, 'playing', 'Launch');
+650|    return true;
+651|  }
+652|
+653|  function handleLaunchKey(state) {
+654|    if (state.mode === 'ready' || state.mode === 'life-lost') {
+655|      return launchWaitingBalls(state);
+656|    }
+657|    if (state.mode === 'paused' && state.balls.some((ball) => ball.state 
=== 'waiting' || ball.state === 'held')) {
+658|      return launchWaitingBalls(state);
+659|    }
+660|    return false;
+661|  }
+662|
+663|  function stepOne(state, input = {}, options = {}) {
+664|    if (!state || state.mode === 'menu') return state;
+665|    const force = Boolean(options.force);
+666|    if (!force && state.mode !== 'playing') {
+667|      if (state.mode === 'ready' || state.mode === 'life-lost') {
+668|        updatePaddle(state, input, STEP, true);
+669|        for (const ball of state.balls) {
+670|          if (ball.state === 'waiting' || ball.state === 'held') {
+671|            ball.x = state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2;
+672|            ball.y = state.paddle.y - ball.radius - 1;
+673|          }
+674|        }
+675|      }
+676|      return state;
+677|    }
+678|    const dt = STEP;
+679|    state.stepCount += 1;
+680|    state.simulationSeconds += dt;
+681|    updatePaddle(state, input, dt, true);
+682|    checkPowerExpiry(state, dt);
+683|    collectDrops(state, dt);
+684|    if (input.launch) handleLaunchKey(state);
+685|    if (state.mode !== 'playing' && !force) return state;
+686|    for (const ball of state.balls) {
+687|      if (ball.state === 'waiting') {
+688|        ball.x = state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2;
+689|        ball.y = state.paddle.y - ball.radius - 1;
+690|        continue;
+691|      }
+692|      if (ball.state === 'held') {
+693|        ball.x = state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2;
+694|        ball.y = state.paddle.y - ball.radius - 1;
+695|        continue;
+696|      }
+697|      if (ball.state !== 'moving') continue;
+698|      const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) || state.baseSpeed;
+699|      const subSteps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((speed * dt) / 2.5));
+700|      const subDt = dt / subSteps;
+701|      let collided = false;
+702|      for (let i = 0; i < subSteps; i += 1) {
+703|        ball.x += ball.vx * subDt;
+704|        ball.y += ball.vy * subDt;
+705|        resolveBallWalls(state, ball);
+706|        if (resolveBallPaddle(state, ball)) {
+707|          collided = true;
+708|          break;
+709|        }
+710|        const bricks = state.bricks.filter((brick) => !brick.destroyed);
+711|        for (const brick of bricks) {
+712|          if (hitBrick(state, ball, brick)) {
+713|            collided = true;
+714|            break;
+715|          }
+716|        }
+717|        if (collided) break;
+718|        if (ball.y - ball.radius > H + BALL_FLOOR_PAD) {
+719|          ballDied(state, ball);
+720|          collided = true;
+721|          break;
+722|        }
+723|      }
+724|      clampBallSpeed(state, ball);
+725|      if (state.mode === 'life-lost' || state.mode === 'game-over' || 
state.mode === 'completed') break;
+726|    }
+727|    removeLostBalls(state);
+728|    if (state.mode === 'playing') {
+729|      const breakable = state.bricks.some((brick) => !brick.destroyed && 
brick.type !== 'solid');
+730|      if (!breakable) completeLevel(state);
+731|    }
+732|    if (state.activePower) state.activePower.remaining = Math.max(0, 
state.activePower.remaining);
+733|    return state;
+734|  }
+735|
+736|  function advanceForDrill(state, seconds = 1, input = {}) {
+737|    const targetSteps = Math.min(120, Math.max(1, Math.round(seconds * 
120)));
+738|    const initialMode = state.mode;
+739|    state.mode = 'playing';
+740|    state.phase = 'playing';
+741|    for (let i = 0; i < targetSteps; i += 1) {
+742|      stepOne(state, input, { force: true });
+743|      if (state.mode === 'life-lost' || state.mode === 'game-over' || 
state.mode === 'level-complete' || state.mode === 'completed') {
+744|        break;
+745|      }
+746|    }
+747|    if (state.mode === 'playing') {
+748|      state.mode = 'paused';
+749|      state.phase = 'paused';
+750|    }
+751|    if (initialMode === 'paused' && state.mode === 'playing') state.mode = 
'paused';
+752|    state.manualControl = false;
+753|    return state;
+754|  }
+755|
+756|  function createNextLevelState(state, runId) {

+832|      timer: Math.max(0, Math.ceil(state.activePower ?

   347    function reflectBallFromBrick(ball, brick) {
   348      const cx = ball.x;
   349      const cy = ball.y;
   350      const rx = brick.x;
   351      const ry = brick.y;
   352      const rw = brick.width;
   353      const rh = brick.height;
   354      const nearestX = clamp(cx, rx, rx + rw);
   355      const nearestY = clamp(cy, ry, ry + rh);
   356      let nx = cx - nearestX;
   357      let ny = cy - nearestY;
   358      if (nx === 0 && ny === 0) {
   359        const left = Math.abs(cx - rx);
   360        const right = Math.abs(cx - (rx + rw));
   361        const top = Math.abs(cy - ry);
   362        const bottom = Math.abs(cy - (ry + rh));
   363        const side = Math.min(left, right, top, bottom);
   364        if (side === left) nx = -1;
   365        else if (side === right) nx = 1;
   366        else if (side === top) ny = -1;
   367        else ny = 1;
   368      }
   369      const length = Math.hypot(nx, ny) || 1;
   370      nx /= length;

   452    function collectDrops(state, dt) {
   453      const paddle = state.paddle;
   454      const reachable = state.assistPaddle ? ASSIST_SPEED : null;
   455      for (const drop of state.drops) {
   456        drop.y += drop.vy * dt;
   457        if (state.assistPaddle && reachable !== null) {
   458          const targetX = drop.x + 10 - paddle.width / 2;
   459          const dx = targetX - paddle.x;
   460          const maxMove = ASSIST_SPEED * dt;
   461          paddle.vx = clamp(dx, -maxMove, maxMove) / dt;
   462          paddle.x += clamp(dx, -maxMove, maxMove);
   463        }
   464        const catchX = paddle.x;
   465        const catchY = paddle.y;
   466        const hit = drop.x + 20 >= catchX && drop.x <= catchX + 
paddle.width && drop.y + 20 >= catchY && drop.y <= catchY + paddle.height;
   467        if (hit) {
   468          applyPower(state, drop.type, drop.type);
   469          drop.collected = true;
   470        }
   471        if (drop.y > H + 30) drop.collected = true;
   472      }
   473      state.drops = state.drops.filter((drop) => !drop.collected);
   474    }

   658    function stepOne(state, input = {}, options = {}) {
   659      if (!state || state.mode === 'menu') return state;
   660      const force = Boolean(options.force);
   661      if (!force && state.mode !== 'playing') {
   662        if (state.mode === 'ready' || state.mode === 'life-lost') {
   663          updatePaddle(state, input, STEP, true);
   664          for (const ball of state.balls) {

   668            }
   669          }

   681      for (const ball of state.balls) {
   682        if (ball.state === 'waiting') {

   692        if (ball.state !== 'moving') continue;
   693        const speed = Math.hypot(ball.vx, ball.vy) || state.baseSpeed;
   694        const subSteps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((speed * dt) / 2.5));
   695        const subDt = dt / subSteps;
   696        let collided = false;
   697        for (let i = 0; i < subSteps; i += 1) {
   698          ball.x += ball.vx * subDt;
   699          ball.y += ball.vy * subDt;
   700          resolveBallWalls(state, ball);
   701          if (resolveBallPaddle(state, ball)) {
   702            collided = true;
   703            break;
   704          }

   706          for (const brick of bricks) {

   709              break;
   710            }
