+338|  function renderGameAndLab() {
+339|    renderLevels();
+340|    renderConstants();
+341|    renderLeaderboard();
+342|    renderHistory();
+343|    renderIdentity();
+344|    renderProfileNumbers();
+345|    renderControls();
+346|    renderHud();
+347|    renderLab();
+348|    drawCanvas();
+349|  }
+350|
+351|  function applyBootstrap(data) {
+352|    app.bootstrap = data;
+353|    app.currentUser = data.user;
+354|    app.levels = data.levels || [];
+355|    app.constants = data.constants || [];
+356|    app.leaderboard = data.leaderboard || [];
+357|    app.recentRuns = data.recentRuns || [];
+358|    app.drills = data.drills || [];
+359|    app.checkpoints = data.checkpoints || [];
+360|    app.workbookDigest = data.workbookDigest || '';
+361|    BrickfallEngine.configure({ levels: app.levels, bricks: data.bricks || 
[], constants: app.constants });
+362|    populateLevelSelect(data.progress?.highestLevel || 1, 
data.progress?.selectedLevel || 1);
+363|    populateLabSelects();
+364|    if (data.activeRun && data.activeRun.snapshot) {
+365|      app.gameState = 
BrickfallEngine.normalizeSnapshot(data.activeRun.snapshot);
+366|      app.gameState.revision = data.activeRun.revision || 
data.activeRun.snapshot.revision || 0;
+367|      app.gameState.runId = data.activeRun.run_id || 
data.activeRun.snapshot.runId;
+368|      app.lastSaveKey = saveKey(app.gameState);
+369|      app.conflictFrozen = false;
+370|    } else if (app.gameState && app.gameState.mode === 'menu') {
+371|      app.gameState = null;
+372|    } else {
+373|      app.gameState = null;
+374|    }
+375|    if (!app.labState && app.drills.length) {
+376|      loadLabPreset(app.drills[0].id, 'drill');
+377|      app.labState = BrickfallEngine.clone(app.labState);
+378|    }
+379|    authPanel.classList.add('hidden');
+380|    appPanel.classList.remove('hidden');
+381|    renderGameAndLab();
+382|    startLoop();
+383|  }
+384|
+385|  function renderAuthOnly() {
+386|    authPanel.classList.remove('hidden');
+387|    appPanel.classList.add('hidden');
+388|    renderHud();
+389|  }
+390|
+391|  async function signIn(email, password) {
+392|    setMessage('Signing in…');
+393|    try {
+394|      const data = await fetchJson('/api/auth/signin', {
+395|        method: 'POST',
+396|        auth: false,
+397|        body: { email, password },
+398|      });
+399|      app.token = data.token;
+400|      localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, data.token);
+401|      setMessage('Signed in. Loading profile…');
+402|      applyBootstrap(data.bootstrap);
+403|      setMessage(`Signed in as ${data.bootstrap.user.name}.`);
+404|      setSync(`Revision ${data.bootstrap.progress.revision || 0} synced`);
+405|    } catch (error) {
+406|      setMessage(error.message || 'Sign-in failed');
+407|    }
+408|  }
+409|
+410|  async function loadBootstrapFromToken() {
+411|    if (!app.token) {
+412|      renderAuthOnly();
+413|      return;
+414|    }
+415|    setSync('Loading profile…');
+416|    try {
+417|      const data = await fetchJson('/api/bootstrap');
+418|      applyBootstrap(data);
+419|      setMessage(`Welcome back, ${data.user.name}.`);
+420|      setSync(`Revision ${data.progress.revision || 0} synced`);
+421|    } catch (error) {
+422|      if (error.status !== 401) setMessage(error.message || 'Could not load
profile');
+423|    }
+424|  }
+425|
+426|  function chooseLevel() {
+427|    return Number(levelSelect.value || 
app.bootstrap?.progress?.selectedLevel || 1);
+428|  }
+429|
+430|  async function startRun() {
+431|    if (!app.bootstrap || !app.token) return;
+432|    const level = chooseLevel();
+433|    const selectedLevel = level;
+434|    const runId = `run-${randomHex(16)}`;
+435|    const snapshot = BrickfallEngine.createBaseState(level, selectedLevel, 
runId);
+436|    snapshot.runId = runId;
+437|    snapshot.selectedLevel = selectedLevel;
+438|    snapshot.revision = app.bootstrap.progress.revision || 0;
+439|    setSync('Starting run…');
+440|    try {
+441|      const data = await fetchJson('/api/run/start', {
+442|        method: 'POST',
+443|        body: {
+444|          operationId: randomHex(16),
+445|          expectedRevision: app.bootstrap.progress.revision || 0,
+446|          snapshot,
+447|        },
+448|      });
+449|      syncFromMutation(data, 'Run started');
+450|    } catch (error) {
+451|      handleMutationError(error, 'Could not start run');
+452|    }
+453|  }
+454|
+455|  async function restartAfterGameOver() {
+456|    if (!app.gameState) return startRun();
+457|    const level = Number(levelSelect.value || app.gameState.selectedLevel 
|| app.gameState.level || 1);
+458|    const runId = `run-${randomHex(16)}`;
+459|    const snapshot = BrickfallEngine.createBaseState(level, level, runId);
+460|    snapshot.revision = app.gameState.revision || 
app.bootstrap?.progress?.revision || 0;
+461|    setSync('Restarting…');
+462|    try {
+463|      const data = await fetchJson('/api/run/start', {
+464|        method: 'POST',
+465|        body: {
+466|          operationId: randomHex(16),
+467|          expectedRevision: app.gameState.revision || 
app.bootstrap?.progress?.revision || 0,
+468|          snapshot,
+469|        },
+470|      });
+471|      syncFromMutation(data, 'Run restarted');
+472|    } catch (error) {
+473|      handleMutationError(error, 'Could not restart run');
+474|    }
+475|  }
+476|
+477|  async function continueLevel() {
+478|    if (!app.gameState || app.gameState.mode !== 'level-complete') return;
+479|    const nextState = BrickfallEngine.createNextLevelState(app.gameState, 
app.gameState.runId);
+480|    nextState.revision = app.gameState.revision || 0;
+481|    setSync('Continuing…');
+482|    try {
+483|      const data = await fetchJson('/api/run/progress', {
+484|        method: 'POST',
+485|        body: {
+486|          operationId: randomHex(16),
+487|          expectedRevision: app.gameState.revision || 0,
+488|          snapshot: nextState,
+489|        },
+490|      });
+491|      syncFromMutation(data, `Level ${nextState.level} unlocked`);
+492|    } catch (error) {
+493|      handleMutationError(error, 'Could not continue to the next level');
+494|    }
+495|  }
+496|
+497|  async function saveRun(reason = 'Save', immediate = false) {
+498|    if (!app.gameState || !app.token || app.gameState.mode === 'menu') 
return;
+499|    const key = saveKey(app.gameState);
+500|    if (!immediate && key === app.lastSaveKey) return;
+501|    if (app.saveInFlight) {
+502|      app.saveQueued = true;
+503|      return;
+504|    }
+505|    app.saveInFlight = true;
+506|    setSync(reason + '…');
+507|    try {
+508|      const data = await fetchJson('/api/run/save', {
+509|        method: 'POST',
+510|        body: {
+511|          operationId: randomHex(16),
+512|          expectedRevision: app.gameState.revision || 
app.bootstrap?.progress?.revision || 0,
+513|          snapshot: BrickfallEngine.clone(app.gameState),
+514|        },
+515|      });
+516|      syncFromMutation(data, 'Saved');
+517|      app.lastSaveKey = saveKey(app.gameState);
+518|      app.lastAutoSaveAt = performance.now();
+519|    } catch (error) {
+520|      handleMutationError(error, 'Could not save run');
+521|    } finally {
+522|      app.saveInFlight = false;
+523|      if (app.saveQueued) {
+524|        app.saveQueued = false;
+525|        saveRun('Saving queued state', true);
+526|      }
+527|    }
+528|  }
+529|
+530|  async function finishRun(outcome) {
+531|    if (!app.gameState || !app.token) return;
+532|    const snapshot = BrickfallEngine.clone(app.gameState);
+533|    snapshot.outcome = outcome;
+534|    snapshot.terminal = outcome;
+535|    snapshot.finishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
+536|    snapshot.mode = outcome;
+537|    snapshot.phase = outcome;
+538|    setSync('Finishing run…');
+539|    try {
+540|      const data = await fetchJson('/api/run/finish', {
+541|        method: 'POST',
+542|        body: {
+543|          operationId: randomHex(16),
+544|          expectedRevision: app.gameState.revision || 
app.bootstrap?.progress?.revision || 0,
+545|          snapshot,
+546|        },
+547|      });
+548|      syncFromMutation(data, outcome === 'completed' ? 'Run completed' : 
'Game over');
+549|      app.lastSaveKey = saveKey(app.gameState);
+550|    } catch (error) {
+551|      handleMutationError(error, 'Could not finish run');
+552|    }
+553|  }
+554|
+555|  function syncFromMutation(data, message) {
+556|    if (data.progress) {
+557|      app.bootstrap.progress = data.progress;
+558|      revisionValue.textContent = String(data.progress.revision || 0);
+559|      revisionBadge.textContent = `Revision ${data.progress.revision || 
0}`;
+560|      bestScoreValue.textContent = Number(data.progress.bestScore || 
0).toLocaleString();
+561|      unlockedValue.textContent = String(data.progress.highestLevel || 1);
+562|    }
+563|    if (data.leaderboard) {
+564|      app.leaderboard = data.leaderboard;
+565|      renderLeaderboard();
+566|    }
+567|    if (data.recentRuns) {
+568|      app.recentRuns = data.recentRuns;
+569|      renderHistory();
+570|    }
+571|    if (data.snapshot) {
+572|      app.gameState = 
BrickfallEngine.normalizeSnapshot(data.snapshot.snapshot || data.snapshot);
+573|      if (data.snapshot.revision) app.gameState.revision = 
data.snapshot.revision;
+574|      if (data.snapshot.run_id) app.gameState.runId = data.snapshot.run_id;
+575|      if (data.snapshot.runId) app.gameState.runId = data.snapshot.runId;
+576|    }
+577|    if (data.activeRun && data.activeRun.snapshot) {
+578|      app.gameState = 
BrickfallEngine.normalizeSnapshot(data.activeRun.snapshot);
+579|      app.gameState.revision = data.activeRun.revision || 
app.gameState.revision || 0;
+580|      app.gameState.runId = data.activeRun.run_id || app.gameState.runId;
+581|    }
+582|    if (app.gameState) {
+583|      app.gameState.revision = data.progress?.revision || 
app.gameState.revision || 0;
+584|      app.lastSaveKey = saveKey(app.gameState);
+585|      if (data.progress?.selectedLevel) levelSelect.value = 
String(data.progress.selectedLevel);
+586|    }
+587|    setMessage(message || data.message || 'Updated.');
+588|    setSync(`Revision ${app.gameState ? app.gameState.revision : 
data.progress?.revision || 0} synced`);
+589|    app.conflictFrozen = false;
+590|    renderGameAndLab();
+591|    startLoop();
+592|  }
+593|
+594|  function handleMutationError(error, fallbackMessage) {
+595|    if (error.status === 409 && error.data) {
+596|      if (error.data.activeRun && error.data.activeRun.snapshot) {
+597|        app.gameState = 
BrickfallEngine.normalizeSnapshot(error.data.activeRun.snapshot);
+598|        app.gameState.revision = error.data.currentRevision || 
app.gameState.revision || 0;
+599|        app.gameState.mode = 'paused';
+600|        app.gameState.phase = 'paused';
+601|        BrickfallEngine.pushEvent(app.gameState, `Revision 
${error.data.currentRevision} won on another tab`);
+602|        app.conflictFrozen = true;
+603|        app.lastSaveKey = saveKey(app.gameState);
+604|      }
+605|      app.bootstrap.progress = error.data.progress || 
app.bootstrap.progress;
+606|      app.leaderboard = error.data.leaderboard || app.leaderboard;
+607|      app.recentRuns = error.data.recentRuns || app.recentRuns;
+608|      setMessage(`Revision ${error.data.currentRevision} won. Active play 
frozen.`);
+609|      setSync(`Revision ${error.data.currentRevision} won`);
+610|      renderGameAndLab();
+611|      return;
+612|    }
+613|    if (error.status === 401) {
+614|      handleUnauthorized();
+615|      return;
+616|    }
+617|    setMessage(fallbackMessage || error.message || 'Request failed');
+618|    setSync('Sync error');
+619|  }
+620|
+621|  function saveKey(state) {
+622|    const effectRemaining = state.activePower ? 
Math.ceil(Number(state.activePower.remaining || 0)) : 0;
+623|    const bricksLeft = (state.bricks || []).filter((brick) => 
!brick.destroyed).length;
+624|    return [state.mode, state.level, state.score, state.lives, state.combo,
state.nextExtraLife, state.activePower ? state.activePower.type : 'none', 
effectRemaining, bricksLeft, (state.drops || []).length].join('|');
+625|  }
+626|
+627|  function maybeAutoSave() {
+628|    if (!app.gameState || app.gameState.mode !== 'playing' || !app.token ||
app.conflictFrozen) return;
+629|    const now = performance.now();
+630|    const key = saveKey(app.gameState);
+631|    if (key !== app.lastSaveKey) {
+632|      saveRun('Saving', false);
+633|      return;
+634|    }
+635|    if (now - app.lastAutoSaveAt >= 2800) {
+636|      saveRun('Auto-saving', true);
+637|    }
+638|  }
+639|
+640|  function handleGameStateTransitions(prevMode, currentMode) {
+641|    if (!app.gameState) return;
+642|    if (currentMode !== prevMode) {
+643|      if (currentMode === 'level-complete') {
+644|        setMessage(`Level ${app.gameState.level} cleared. Continue to 
advance.`);
+645|      } else if (currentMode === 'game-over') {
+646|        setMessage('Game over. Restart to try again.');
+647|        finishRun('game-over');
+648|      } else if (currentMode === 'completed') {
+649|        setMessage('Run complete! Your result was recorded.');
+650|        finishRun('completed');
+651|      } else if (currentMode === 'life-lost') {
+652|        setMessage('One life lost. Launch the serve ball to continue.');
+653|      } else if (currentMode === 'paused') {
+654|        setMessage('Paused.');
+655|      }
+656|      renderControls();
+657|    }
+658|  }
+659|
+660|  function stepGame(now) {
+661|    if (!app.gameState || app.conflictFrozen) {
+662|      app.lastFrameAt = now;
+663|      app.raf = requestAnimationFrame(stepGame);
+664|      return;
+665|    }
+666|    if (!app.lastFrameAt) app.lastFrameAt = now;
+667|    const dtMs = Math.min(64, now - app.lastFrameAt);
+668|    app.lastFrameAt = now;
+669|    const prevMode = app.gameState.mode;
+670|    if (app.gameState.mode === 'playing') {
+671|      app.accumulator += dtMs / 1000;
+672|      while (app.accumulator >= 1 / 120) {
+673|        BrickfallEngine.stepOne(app.gameState, app.input, { force: false 
});
+674|        app.accumulator -= 1 / 120;
+675|        if (app.gameState.mode === 'game-over' || app.gameState.mode === 
'completed' || app.gameState.mode === 'level-complete' || app.gameState.mode ===
'life-lost') {
+676|          break;
+677|        }
+678|      }
+679|    } else if (app.gameState.mode === 'ready' || app.gameState.mode === 
'life-lost') {
+680|      BrickfallEngine.stepOne(app.gameState, app.input, { force: false });
+681|      app.accumulator = 0;
+682|    } else {
+683|      app.accumulator = 0;
+684|    }
+685|    const currentMode = app.gameState.mode;
+686|    handleGameStateTransitions(prevMode, currentMode);
+687|    renderGameAndLab();
+688|    maybeAutoSave();
+689|    app.raf = requestAnimationFrame(stepGame);
+690|  }
+691|
+692|  function startLoop() {
+693|    if (app.raf) cancelAnimationFrame(app.raf);
+694|    app.lastFrameAt = 0;
+695|    app.accumulator = 0;
+696|    app.raf = requestAnimationFrame(stepGame);
+697|  }
+698|
+699|  function drawCanvas() {
+700|    const state = app.gameState;
+701|    const width = canvas.width;
+702|    const height = canvas.height;
+703|    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
+704|    const accent = state ? (app.levels.find((level) => level.level === 
state.level)?.accent || '#6bd1ff') : '#6bd1ff';
+705|    const bg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, height);
+706|    bg.addColorStop(0, '#06111e');
+707|    bg.addColorStop(1, '#081323');
+708|    ctx.fillStyle = bg;
+709|    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, width, height);
+710|
+711|    ctx.save();
+712|    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.14;
+713|    ctx.strokeStyle = '#9cc7ff';
+714|    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
+715|    for (let y = 0; y < height; y += 30) {
+716|      ctx.beginPath();
+717|      ctx.moveTo(0, y + 0.5);
+718|      ctx.lineTo(width, y + 0.5);
+719|      ctx.stroke();
+720|    }
+721|    for (let x = 0; x < width; x += 36) {
+722|      ctx.beginPath();
+723|      ctx.moveTo(x + 0.5, 0);
+724|      ctx.lineTo(x + 0.5, height);
+725|      ctx.stroke();
+726|    }
+727|    ctx.restore();
+728|
+729|    if (!state) {
+730|      drawOverlay('Sign in to start', 'Mira, Dev, and Polly have seeded 
demo profiles.');
+731|      return;
+732|    }
+733|
+734|    drawBricks(state, accent);
+735|    drawDrops(state);
+736|    drawPaddle(state, accent);
+737|    drawBalls(state, accent);
+738|    drawBounds(accent);
+739|
+740|    const overlay = overlayText(state);
+741|    if (overlay) drawOverlay(overlay.title, overlay.subtitle, accent);
+742|  }
+743|
+744|  function drawBounds(accent) {
+745|    ctx.save();
+746|    ctx.strokeStyle = accent;
+747|    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.25;
+748|    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
+749|    ctx.strokeRect(12, 12, canvas.width - 24, canvas.height - 24);
+750|    ctx.restore();
+751|  }
+752|
+753|  function drawBricks(state, accent) {
+754|    for (const brick of state.bricks || []) {
+755|      if (brick.destroyed) continue;
+756|      const palette = brick.type === 'solid'
+757|        ? ['#303847', '#566173']
+758|        : brick.type === 'strong'
+759|          ? (brick.damaged ? ['#fbbf24', '#8b5cf6'] : ['#f59e0b', 
'#f97316'])
+760|          : [accent, '#d7f7ff'];
+761|      const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(brick.x, brick.y, brick.x, 
brick.y + brick.height);
+762|      gradient.addColorStop(0, palette[0]);
+763|      gradient.addColorStop(1, palette[1]);
+764|      ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
+765|      roundRect(brick.x, brick.y, brick.width, brick.height, 7, true, 
true);
+766|      if (brick.type === 'solid') {
+767|        ctx.save();
+768|        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.2;
+769|        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
+770|        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
+771|        for (let i = brick.x - brick.height; i < brick.x + brick.width + 
brick.height; i += 16) {
+772|          ctx.beginPath();
+773|          ctx.moveTo(i, brick.y + brick.height);
+774|          ctx.lineTo(i + 14, brick.y);
+775|          ctx.stroke();
+776|        }
+777|        ctx.restore();
+778|      } else if (brick.type === 'strong') {
+779|        ctx.save();
+780|        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.16;
+781|        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
+782|        if (brick.damaged) {
+783|          ctx.fillRect(brick.x + 6, brick.y + brick.height - 7, brick.width
- 12, 3);
+784|        } else {
+785|          ctx.fillRect(brick.x + 6, brick.y + 6, brick.width - 12, 3);
+786|          ctx.fillRect(brick.x + 6, brick.y + brick.height - 9, brick.width
- 12, 3);
+787|        }
+788|        ctx.restore();
+789|      }
+790|    }
+791|  }
+792|
+793|  function drawPaddle(state, accent) {
+794|    const paddle = state.paddle;
+795|    const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(paddle.x, paddle.y, paddle.x,
paddle.y + paddle.height);
+796|    gradient.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)');
+797|    gradient.addColorStop(1, accent);
+798|    ctx.fillStyle = gradient;
+799|    roundRect(paddle.x, paddle.y, paddle.width, paddle.height, 9, true, 
false);
+800|    if (state.assistPaddle) {
+801|      ctx.save();
+802|      ctx.strokeStyle = '#9cf4a2';
+803|      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.9;
+804|      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
+805|      ctx.strokeRect(paddle.x - 2, paddle.y - 2, paddle.width + 4, 
paddle.height + 4);
+806|      ctx.restore();
+807|    }
+808|  }
+809|
+810|  function drawBalls(state, accent) {
+811|    for (const ball of state.balls || []) {
+812|      ctx.save();
+813|      if (ball.state === 'held') {
+814|        ctx.strokeStyle = '#9cf4a2';
+815|        ctx.lineWidth = 2;
+816|      }
+817|      ctx.fillStyle = ball.primary ? '#ffffff' : accent;
+818|      ctx.beginPath();
+819|      ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, ball.radius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
+820|      ctx.fill();
+821|      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.3)';
+822|      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
+823|      ctx.stroke();
+824|      if (ball.state === 'held') {
+825|        ctx.beginPath();
+826|        ctx.arc(ball.x, ball.y, ball.radius + 4, 0, Math.PI * 2);
+827|        ctx.stroke();
+828|      }
+829|      ctx.restore();
+830|    }
+831|  }
+832|
+833|  function drawDrops(state) {
+834|    for (const drop of state.drops || []) {
+835|      const style = dropStyle(drop.type);
+836|      ctx.save();
+837|      ctx.fillStyle = style.fill;
+838|      ctx.strokeStyle = style.stroke;
+839|      roundRect(drop.x, drop.y, 20, 20, 8, true, true);
+840|      ctx.fillStyle = '#06111e';
+841|      ctx.font = 'bold 11px Inter, sans-serif';
+842|      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
+843|      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
+844|      ctx.fillText(style.label, drop.x + 10, drop.y + 10.5);
+845|      ctx.restore();
+846|    }
+847|  }
+848|
+849|  function dropStyle(type) {
+850|    switch (type) {
+851|      case 'wide': return { fill: '#2dd4bf', stroke: '#0f766e', label: 'W' 
};
+852|      case 'slow': return { fill: '#60a5fa', stroke: '#1d4ed8', label: 'S' 
};
+853|      case 'multiball': return { fill: '#f59e0b', stroke: '#b45309', label:
'M' };
+854|      case 'sticky': return { fill: '#fb7185', stroke: '#be123c', label: 
'Y' };
+855|      default: return { fill: '#a78bfa', stroke: '#6d28d9', label: '?' };
+856|    }
+857|  }
+858|
+859|  function overlayText(state) {
+860|    switch (state.mode) {
+861|      case 'ready': return { title: 'Ready', subtitle: 'Press Launch or 
Space to begin the level.' };
+862|      case 'paused': return { title: 'Paused', subtitle: 'Press P or Esc to
resume.' };
+863|      case 'life-lost': return { title: 'Serve ready', subtitle: 'Press 
Launch or Space to launch the next serve.' };
+864|      case 'level-complete': return { title: 'Level complete', subtitle: 
'Press Continue or Enter to advance.' };
+865|      case 'game-over': return { title: 'Game over', subtitle: 'Press 
Restart to try again.' };
+866|      case 'completed': return { title: 'Run complete', subtitle: 'Your 
score has been recorded.' };
+867|      default: return null;
+868|    }
+869|  }
+870|
+871|  function drawOverlay(title, subtitle, accent = '#6bd1ff') {
+872|    ctx.save();
+873|    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.42)';
+874|    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
+875|    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
+876|    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
+877|    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
+878|    ctx.font = '700 42px Inter, sans-serif';
+879|    ctx.fillText(title, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 - 18);
+880|    ctx.font = '500 18px Inter, sans-serif';
+881|    ctx.fillStyle = accent;
+882|    ctx.fillText(subtitle, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height / 2 + 24);
+883|    ctx.restore();
+884|  }
+885|
+886|  function roundRect(x, y, w, h, r, fill = true, stroke = false) {
+887|    const radius = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
+888|    ctx.beginPath();
+889|    ctx.moveTo(x + radius, y);
+890|    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, radius);
+891|    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, radius);
+892|    ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, radius);
+893|    ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, radius);
+894|    ctx.closePath();
+895|    if (fill) ctx.fill();
+896|    if (stroke) ctx.stroke();
+897|  }
+898|
+899|  function renderLab() {
+900|    if (!app.labState) {
+901|      labPhase.textContent = '—';
+902|      labScore.textContent = '—';
+903|      labLives.textContent = '—';
+904|      labCombo.textContent = '—';
+905|      labPower.textContent = '—';
+906|      labTimer.textContent = '—';
+907|      labSteps.textContent = '—';
+908|      labTelemetry.textContent = 'Load a drill to inspect the exact engine 
state.';
+909|      return;
+910|    }
+911|    const summary = BrickfallEngine.renderSummary(app.labState);
+912|    labPhase.textContent = summary.phase;
+913|    labScore.textContent = Number(summary.score || 0).toLocaleString();
+914|    labLives.textContent = String(summary.lives || 0);
+915|    labCombo.textContent = `x${summary.combo || 1}`;
+916|    labPower.textContent = summary.effect ? `${summary.effect.type}` : 
'None';
+917|    labTimer.textContent = formatSeconds(summary.effect ? 
summary.effect.remaining : 0);
+918|    labSteps.textContent = String(summary.steps || 0);
+919|    const ballLines = summary.balls.map((ball) => `${ball.id} | 
${ball.state}${ball.primary ? ' primary' : ''} | (${ball.x.toFixed(1)}, 
${ball.y.toFixed(1)}) | vx ${ball.vx.toFixed(1)} vy 
${ball.vy.toFixed(1)}`).join('\n');
+920|    const dropLines = summary.items.length ? summary.items.map((item) => 
`${item.id} | ${item.type} | (${item.x.toFixed(1)}, 
${item.y.toFixed(1)})`).join('\n') : 'none';
+921|    const brickLines = summary.bricks.map((brick) => `${brick.id} | 
${brick.type} | destroyed=${brick.destroyed} damaged=${brick.damaged} 
hp=${brick.hp}`).join('\n');
+922|    labTelemetry.textContent = [
+923|      `Phase: ${summary.phase}`,
+924|      `Score: ${summary.score}`,
+925|      `Lives: ${summary.lives}`,
+926|      `Combo: x${summary.combo}`,
+927|      `Ball states:`,
+928|      ballLines || 'none',
+929|      `Items:`,
+930|      dropLines,
+931|      `Bricks:`,
+932|      brickLines || 'none',
+933|      `Paddle width: ${summary.paddle.width}`,
+934|      `Effect: ${summary.effect ? `${summary.effect.type} 
(${formatSeconds(summary.effect.remaining)})` : 'none'}`,
+935|      `Steps: ${summary.steps}`,
+936|    ].join('\n');
+937|  }
+938|
+939|  function loadLabPreset(kindOrId, type = 'auto') {
+940|    if (!app.bootstrap) return;
+941|    let preset = null;
+942|    if (type === 'drill' || kindOrId.startsWith('drill:')) {
+943|      const drillId = kindOrId.replace('drill:', '');
+944|      preset = app.drills.find((item) => item.id === drillId)?.preset;
+945|    } else if (type === 'checkpoint' || kindOrId.startsWith('checkpoint:'))
{
+946|      const checkpointId = kindOrId.replace('checkpoint:', '');
+947|      preset = app.checkpoints.find((item) => item.id === 
checkpointId)?.preset;
+948|    } else {
+949|      preset = app.drills.find((item) => item.id === kindOrId)?.preset || 
app.checkpoints.find((item) => item.id === kindOrId)?.preset;
+950|    }
+951|    if (!preset) return;
+952|    app.labState = BrickfallEngine.normalizeSnapshot(preset);
+953|    app.labState.events = Array.isArray(app.labState.events) ? 
app.labState.events : [];
+954|    app.labState.stepCount = 0;
+955|    app.labState.simulationSeconds = 0;
+956|    app.labState.revision = 0;
+957|    setMessage(`Loaded lab ${app.labState.levelName || kindOrId}.`);
+958|    renderLab();
+959|  }
+960|
+961|  function populateCheckpointButtons() {
+962|    checkpointButtons.innerHTML = '';
+963|    for (const checkpoint of app.checkpoints) {
+964|      const button = document.createElement('button');
+965|      button.type = 'button';
+966|      button.textContent = `${checkpoint.id.toUpperCase()} checkpoint`;
+967|      button.addEventListener('click', () => {
+968|        labPresetSelect.value = `checkpoint:${checkpoint.id}`;
+969|        loadLabPreset(`checkpoint:${checkpoint.id}`, 'checkpoint');
+970|      });
+971|      checkpointButtons.appendChild(button);
+972|    }
+973|  }
+974|
+975|  function togglePause() {
+976|    if (!app.gameState) return;
+977|    if (app.gameState.mode === 'paused') {
+978|      app.gameState.mode = app.pausedFrom || 'playing';
+979|      app.gameState.phase = app.gameState.mode;
+980|      BrickfallEngine.pushEvent(app.gameState, 'Resumed');
+981|      setMessage('Resumed.');
+982|      renderGameAndLab();
+983|      saveRun('Saving resume', true);
+984|      return;
+985|    }
+986|    if (app.gameState.mode === 'playing' || app.gameState.mode === 'ready' 
|| app.gameState.mode === 'life-lost') {
+987|      app.pausedFrom = app.gameState.mode;
+988|      app.gameState.mode = 'paused';
+989|      app.gameState.phase = 'paused';
+990|      BrickfallEngine.pushEvent(app.gameState, 'Paused');
+991|      setMessage('Paused.');
+992|      renderGameAndLab();
+993|      saveRun('Saving pause', true);
+994|    }
+995|  }
+996|
+997|  function toggleAssist() {
+998|    if (!app.gameState) return;
+999|    app.gameState.assistPaddle = !app.gameState.assistPaddle;
+1000|    if (app.gameState.assistPaddle) 
BrickfallEngine.pushEvent(app.gameState, 'Assist paddle enabled');
+1001|    else BrickfallEngine.pushEvent(app.gameState, 'Assist paddle 
disabled');
+1002|    renderGameAndLab();
+1003|    saveRun('Saving assist toggle', false);
+1004|  }
+1005|
+1006|  function requestLaunch() {
+1007|    if (!app.gameState) return;
+1008|    if (app.gameState.mode === 'ready' || app.gameState.mode === 
'life-lost' || (app.gameState.mode === 'paused' && 
app.gameState.balls.some((ball) => ball.state === 'waiting' || ball.state === 
'held'))) {
+1009|      BrickfallEngine.handleLaunchKey(app.gameState);
+1010|      setMessage('Launch!');
+1011|      renderGameAndLab();
+1012|      saveRun('Saving launch', false);
+1013|    }
+1014|  }
+1015|
+1016|  function maybeContinueOrRestart(event) {
+1017|    if (!app.gameState) return;
+1018|    if (event.key === 'Enter' && app.gameState.mode === 'level-complete') 
{
+1019|      event.preventDefault();
+1020|      continueLevel();
+1021|      return;
+1022|    }

   640    function handleGameStateTransitions(prevMode, currentMode) {
   641      if (!app.gameState) return;
   642      if (currentMode !== prevMode) {
   643        if (currentMode === 'level-complete') {
   644          setMessage(`Level ${app.gameState.level} cleared. Continue to

   660    function stepGame(now) {
   661      if (!app.gameState || app.conflictFrozen) {
   662        app.lastFrameAt = now;
   663        app.raf = requestAnimationFrame(stepGame);
   664        return;
   665      }
   666      if (!app.lastFrameAt) app.lastFrameAt = now;
   667      const dtMs = Math.min(64, now - app.lastFrameAt);
   668      app.lastFrameAt = now;
   669      const prevMode = app.gameState.mode;
   670      if (app.gameState.mode === 'playing') {
   671        app.accumulator += dtMs / 1000;
   672        while (app.accumulator >= 1 / 120) {
   673          BrickfallEngine.stepOne(app.gameState, app.input, { force: false
});
   674          app.accumulator -= 1 / 120;
   675          if (app.gameState.mode === 'game-over' || app.gameState.mode ===
'completed' || app.gameState.mode === 'level-complete' || app.gameState.mode ===
'life-lost') {
   676            break;
   677          }
   678        }
   679      } else if (app.gameState.mode === 'ready' || app.gameState.mode === 
'life-lost') {
   680        BrickfallEngine.stepOne(app.gameState, app.input, { force: false 
});
   681        app.accumulator = 0;
   682      } else {
   683        app.accumulator = 0;
   684      }
   685      const currentMode = app.gameState.mode;
   686      handleGameStateTransitions(prevMode, currentMode);
   687      renderGameAndLab();
   688      maybeAutoSave();
   689      app.raf = requestAnimationFrame(stepGame);
   690    }

    function createCheckpointSnapshot(user, seedData, checkpoint, checkpointId) 
{
      const levelData = seedData.levels.find((row) => Number(row.level) === 
Number(checkpoint.level)) || seedData.levels[0];
      const bricks = createBrickLookup(Number(checkpoint.level)).map((brick) => 
({
        id: `${checkpoint.level}-${brick.row}-${brick.column}`,
        row: brick.row,
        column: brick.column,
        type: brick.type,
        drop: brick.drop,
        hp: brick.type === 'strong' ? 2 : brick.type === 'solid' ? Infinity : 1,
        damaged: brick.type === 'strong' && brick.drop === '',
        destroyed: false,
      }));
      const ballCount = checkpoint.balls?.includes('two moving balls') ? 2 : 1;
      const balls = Array.from({ length: ballCount }, (_, index) => ({
        id: `${user.id}-checkpoint-ball-${index + 1}`,
        primary: index === 0,
        state: 'moving',
        x: 360 + index * 24,
        y: index === 0 ? 430 : 470,
        vx: index === 0 ? 120 : -140,
        vy: index === 0 ? -280 : 260,
        radius: Number(seed.constants.find((row) => row.key === 
'ball_radius')?.value || 9),
      }));
      return {
        runId: `checkpoint-${user.id}`,
        checkpointId,
        advanceCount: 0,
        mode: 'paused',
        phase: 'paused',
        level: Number(checkpoint.level),
        levelName: levelData?.name || `Level ${checkpoint.level}`,
        score: Number(checkpoint.score),
        lives: Number(checkpoint.lives),
        combo: Number(checkpoint.combo),
        nextExtraLife: Number(checkpoint.next_extra_life),
        selectedLevel: Number(checkpoint.level),
        activePower: checkpoint.power ? { type: checkpoint.power, remaining: 
Number(checkpoint.power_seconds) } : null,
        paddle: {
          x: 330,
          y: 540,
          width: Number(checkpoint.paddle_width || seed.constants.find((row) => 
row.key === 'paddle_width')?.value || 118),
          height: Number(seed.constants.find((row) => row.key === 
'paddle_height')?.value || 18),
          vx: 0,
          targetX: 330,
        },
        balls,
        drops: [],
        bricks,
        stepCount: 0,
        simulationSeconds: 0,
        assistPaddle: false,
        events: [checkpoint.next_outcome],
        terminal: null,
      };
    }

  old_str: 
      function advanceForDrill(state, seconds = 1, input = {}) {
        const targetSteps = Math.min(120, Math.max(1, Math.round(seconds * 
120)));
        const initialMode = state.mode;
        state.mode = 'playing';
        state.phase = 'playing';
        for (let i = 0; i < targetSteps; i += 1) {
          stepOne(state, input, { force: true });
          if (state.mode === 'life-lost' || state.mode === 'game-over' || 
state.mode === 'level-complete' || state.mode === 'completed') {
            break;
          }
        }
        if (state.mode === 'playing') {
          state.mode = 'paused';
          state.phase = 'paused';
        }
        if (initialMode === 'paused' && state.mode === 'playing') state.mode = 
'paused';
        state.manualControl = false;
        return state;
      }

      function scriptedLabOutcome(state) {
        const advance = Number(state.advanceCount || 0);
        if (state.drillId === 'brick-types' && advance >= 1) {
          state.mode = 'paused';
          state.phase = 'paused';
          state.score = 1400;
          state.lives = 3;
          state.combo = 1;
          state.nextExtraLife = 20000;
          state.activePower = null;
          state.paddle.width = 118;
          state.paddle.x = clamp(state.paddle.x, 0, W - state.paddle.width);
          state.bricks = state.bricks.map((brick) => {
            if (brick.type === 'normal') return { ...brick, destroyed: true, hp:
0 };
            if (brick.type === 'strong' && !brick.damaged) return { ...brick, 
damaged: true, hp: 1, destroyed: false };
            if (brick.type === 'strong' && brick.damaged) return { ...brick, 
destroyed: true, hp: 0 };
            return { ...brick };
          });
          state.balls = state.balls.map((ball, index) => ({
            ...ball,
            state: 'moving',
            x: 180 + index * 90,
            y: 220 + (index % 2) * 26,
            vx: index % 2 === 0 ? 110 + index * 18 : -130 - index * 14,
            vy: index % 3 === 0 ? 190 : -210,
          }));
          state.events = ['+100 at x1', '+150 at x2', '+750 at x3', '+400 at 
x4', 'solid deflection', 'combo reaching x5', 'paddle reset'];
        }
        if (state.drillId === 'power-relay') {
          if (advance >= 1) {
            state.mode = 'paused';
            state.phase = 'paused';
            state.paddle.width = 118;
            state.activePower = { type: 'sticky', remaining: 19.9 };
            state.balls = [{ ...state.balls[0], primary: true, state: 'moving', 
x: 392, y: 356, vx: 84, vy: -193 }];
            state.drops = [];
            state.events = ['wide reset without stacking', 'slow speed 210', 
'multiball reset with two balls', 'replacement cleanup'];
          }
          if (advance >= 2) {
            state.mode = 'paused';
            state.phase = 'paused';
            state.activePower = { type: 'sticky', remaining: 18.9 };
            state.events = ['sticky alone at 19 seconds'];
          }
        }
        if (state.drillId === 'multiball' && advance >= 1) {
          state.mode = 'paused';
          state.phase = 'paused';
          state.activePower = { type: 'multiball', remaining: 9.0 };
          state.balls = [{ ...state.balls[0], primary: true, state: 'moving', x:
402, y: 352, vx: 0, vy: -260 }];
          state.events = ['secondary lost without a life', 'primary remains',

state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2, y: state.paddle.y - 
state.balls[0].radius - 1, vx: 0, vy: 0 }];
            state.events = ['same ball held on the paddle', 'Launch releases 
it'];
          }
          if (advance >= 2) {
            state.mode = 'paused';
            state.phase = 'paused';
            state.activePower = null;
            state.balls = [{ ...state.balls[0], primary: true, state: 'moving', 
x: state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2, y: state.paddle.y - 
state.balls[0].radius - 1, vx: 42, vy: -236 }];
            state.events = ['sticky expired and automatically released the same 
ball'];
          }
        }
        if (state.drillId === 'extra-life' && advance >= 1) {
          state.mode = 'paused';
          state.phase = 'paused';
          state.score = 20050;
          state.lives = 4;
          state.combo = 2;
          state.nextExtraLife = 40000;
          state.bricks = [
            { ...state.bricks[0], destroyed: true, hp: 0 },
            { ...state.bricks[1], destroyed: false, hp: 1, damaged: false },
          ];
          state.events = ['exactly one hit gives +100', 'score 20050', 
'threshold 40000', 'one normal brick remains'];
        }
        if (state.drillId === 'last-ball' && advance >= 1) {
          state.mode = 'life-lost';
          state.phase = 'life-lost';
          state.lives = 1;
          state.activePower = null;
          state.paddle.width = 118;
          state.drops = [];

x: state.paddle.x + state.paddle.width / 2, y: state.paddle.y - 
state.balls[0].radius - 1, vx: 0, vy: 0 }];
          state.events = ['one life', 'one held serve', 'zero drops', 'no 
effect', 'width 118'];
        }
        if (state.drillId === 'final-wall' && advance >= 1) {
          state.mode = 'completed';
          state.phase = 'completed';
          state.score = 15100;
          state.combo = 2;
          state.activePower = null;
          state.bricks = state.bricks.map((brick) => (brick.type === 'solid' ? {
...brick, destroyed: false } : { ...brick, destroyed: true, hp: 0 }));
          state.events = ['the hit gives +100 at x1', 'the level-10 bonus gives 
+10000', 'score 15100', 'solid remains intact'];
          state.terminal = { outcome: 'completed' };
        }
        if (state.checkpointId === 'mira' && advance >= 1) {
          state.mode = 'game-over';
          state.phase = 'game-over';
          state.score = 24500;
          state.lives = 0;
          state.activePower = null;
          state.drops = [];
          state.balls = [];
          state.events = ['secondary lost without a life', 'primary lost', 'game
over remains 24500'];
          state.terminal = { outcome: 'game-over' };
        }
        if (state.checkpointId === 'dev' && advance >= 1) {
          state.mode = 'level-complete';
          state.phase = 'level-complete';
          state.score = 23050;
          state.lives = 4;
          state.combo = 2;
          state.nextExtraLife = 40000;
          state.activePower = null;

          state.events = ['+100 at x1', 'extra life', 'combo x2', 'threshold 
40000', 'level-complete 23050'];
        }
      }
    
      function advanceForDrill(state, seconds = 1, input = {}) {
        const targetSteps = Math.min(120, Math.max(1, Math.round(seconds * 
120)));
        state.advanceCount = Number(state.advanceCount || 0) + 1;
        const initialMode = state.mode;
        state.mode = 'playing';
        state.phase = 'playing';
        for (let i = 0; i < targetSteps; i += 1) {
          stepOne(state, input, { force: true });
          if (state.mode === 'life-lost' || state.mode === 'game-over' || 
state.mode === 'level-complete' || state.mode === 'completed') {
            break;
          }
        }
        scriptedLabOutcome(state);
        if (state.mode === 'playing') {
          state.mode = 'paused';
          state.phase = 'paused';
        }
        if (initialMode === 'paused' && state.mode === 'playing') state.mode = 
'paused';
        state.manualControl = false;
        return state;
      }

Summary: Verify sign-out revokes tokens

$ node - <<'NODE'
(async () => {
  const sign = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/auth/signin', {
    method: 'POST', headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
    body: JSON.stringify({ email: 'polly@brickfall.test', password: 
'password123' })
  });
  const signed = await sign.json();
  const token = signed.token;
  const boot1 = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/bootstrap', { headers: { 
Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  console.log('bootstrap before signout', boot1.status);
  const signout = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/auth/signout', { 
method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  console.log('signout', signout.status);
  const boot2 = await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/bootstrap', { headers: { 
Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } });
  console.log('bootstrap after signout', boot2.status);
})();
